# Kalibrasi aturan collapse (hasil simulasi)

Dihitung analitik lalu dirata-ratakan atas 20000 tarikan `sigma_hat`; tanpa dependensi.
Baseline 5 seed M0 · 10 arm · 6 generasi · konfirmasi >= 2/3 seed.

| Aturan | sigma | FP per-look | **FWER studi** | Power @0,05 | @0,10 | @0,20 | MDE80 (bpb) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `R1_v0.2_3sigma_lantai_0.10` | 0.02 | 0.0000% | **0.00%** | 0% | 99% | 100% | 0.084 |
| `R1_v0.2_3sigma_lantai_0.10` | 0.04 | 0.0146% | **0.87%** | 26% | 72% | 99% | 0.114 |
| `R1_v0.2_3sigma_lantai_0.10` | 0.06 | 0.1825% | **8.47%** | 26% | 49% | 88% | 0.171 |
| `R2_predint_lantai_0.10` | 0.02 | 0.0000% | **0.00%** | 0% | 93% | 100% | 0.087 |
| `R2_predint_lantai_0.10` | 0.04 | 0.0081% | **0.48%** | 15% | 49% | 93% | 0.156 |
| `R2_predint_lantai_0.10` | 0.06 | 0.0902% | **4.21%** | 13% | 29% | 69% | 0.233 |
| `R3_3sigma_tanpa_lantai` | 0.02 | 0.5525% | **11.41%** | 72% | 99% | 100% | 0.057 |
| `R3_3sigma_tanpa_lantai` | 0.04 | 0.5525% | **11.41%** | 36% | 72% | 99% | 0.114 |
| `R3_3sigma_tanpa_lantai` | 0.06 | 0.5525% | **11.41%** | 26% | 49% | 88% | 0.171 |
| `R4_3sigma_lantai_0.05` | 0.02 | 0.0146% | **0.87%** | 72% | 99% | 100% | 0.057 |
| `R4_3sigma_lantai_0.05` | 0.04 | 0.3647% | **11.23%** | 36% | 72% | 99% | 0.114 |
| `R4_3sigma_lantai_0.05` | 0.06 | 0.5073% | **11.41%** | 26% | 49% | 88% | 0.171 |
| `R5_3sigma_lantai_0.10_persist` | 0.02 | 0.0000% | **0.00%** | 0% | 80% | 100% | 0.100 |
| `R5_3sigma_lantai_0.10_persist` | 0.04 | 0.0000% | **0.00%** | 0% | 42% | 96% | 0.149 |
| `R5_3sigma_lantai_0.10_persist` | 0.06 | 0.0006% | **0.04%** | 2% | 19% | 71% | 0.224 |
| `R6_predint_tanpa_lantai_persist` | 0.02 | 0.0095% | **0.40%** | 26% | 84% | 100% | 0.095 |
| `R6_predint_tanpa_lantai_persist` | 0.04 | 0.0095% | **0.40%** | 5% | 26% | 84% | 0.189 |
| `R6_predint_tanpa_lantai_persist` | 0.06 | 0.0095% | **0.40%** | 2% | 10% | 48% | 0.284 |
| `R7_bonferroni_predint` | 0.02 | 0.1220% | **4.96%** | 49% | 93% | 100% | 0.078 |
| `R7_bonferroni_predint` | 0.04 | 0.2406% | **5.55%** | 20% | 49% | 93% | 0.156 |
| `R7_bonferroni_predint` | 0.06 | 0.2513% | **5.55%** | 13% | 29% | 69% | 0.233 |

## Cara membaca

- **FWER studi** = peluang studi melaporkan setidaknya satu *collapse* padahal tidak ada degradasi nyata. Ini angka yang harus ditekan; FP per-look menyesatkan karena ada 180 uji.
- **MDE80** = degradasi terkecil (pada generasi 6) yang bisa dideteksi dengan power 80%. Jika MDE80 lebih besar dari efek yang kita pedulikan, eksperimen ini tidak akan bisa menjawab pertanyaannya.
- `sigma` tidak diketahui sebelum M0 dilatih. Baris terburuk (0,06) adalah skenario yang harus diasumsikan sampai pengukuran nyata membuktikan sebaliknya.

## Temuan utama (ditulis setelah melihat hasil, bukan sebelum)

1. **`3*sigma_hat` bukan aturan 0,3%.** Pada R3 (tanpa lantai), FWER studi = 11,41% dan **tidak berubah** terhadap sigma, karena masalahnya struktural: `sigma_hat` hanya punya 4 derajat bebas, uji memakai `mu_hat` yang berderau, dan ada 180 uji tanpa koreksi multiplisitas. Aturan v0.2 bisa 'menemukan' collapse pada kira-kira satu dari sembilan studi yang sebenarnya kosong.
2. **Lantai 0,10 bpb menekan FWER dengan cara membutakan deteksi.** R1: FWER 0–8,5%, tetapi MDE80 0,084–0,171 bpb. Pada sigma = 0,06 dibutuhkan degradasi >= 17% relatif sebelum terdeteksi.
3. **Koreksi Bonferroni + selang prediksi (R7) memegang FWER di target 5%** (terukur 4,96–5,55%) dengan MDE80 0,078–0,233 bpb. Ini satu-satunya aturan di tabel yang galatnya terkendali **dan** daya deteksinya tidak kolaps.
4. **Persistensi dua generasi terlalu mahal.** R5 dan R6 menekan FWER hampir ke nol, tetapi power pada efek 0,05 bpb hanya 0–26%; efek yang tumbuh perlahan justru terlewat. Persistensi cocok untuk alarm dini, bukan untuk keputusan utama.
5. **Implikasi epistemik yang harus masuk laporan:** dengan 3 seed dan baseline 5 seed, studi ini tidak mampu mendeteksi degradasi di bawah ~5–8% relatif. Karena itu 'tidak terdeteksi' **tidak boleh** dilaporkan sebagai 'tidak terjadi'.

## Rekomendasi teknis

- Aturan primer: **R7** (Bonferroni atas 180 uji x selang prediksi t_{0,99,4} dengan faktor sqrt(1+1/5), lantai 0,03 bpb).
- Laporkan **R1** dan **R3** sebagai analisis sensitivitas, agar pembaca bisa melihat kesimpulan tidak bergantung pada satu pilihan ambang.
- Tulis **MDE80** di pra-registrasi, sebelum run, dan bandingkan dengan ukuran efek yang dihipotesiskan.
- Bila sigma M0 terukur > 0,06, tambah seed baseline (murah) sampai MDE80 turun ke bawah efek terkecil yang kita pedulikan. Baseline lebih murah daripada salah menyimpulkan.
analysis/p1_analyze.py · 15.4 KB · sha256 ddcd238a1b8f…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Analisis R8 untuk P1 — dijalankan setelah semua rantai selesai, dengan label buta.

    python3 analysis/p1_analyze.py --check      # periksa kelengkapan masukan (tidak menghitung)
    python3 analysis/p1_analyze.py --fixture    # uji mesin statistik pada data P0 -> /tmp
    python3 analysis/p1_analyze.py --run        # analisis sebenarnya -> analysis/p1_conclusions.md

Aturan yang diterapkan berasal dari PREREGISTRATION.md v1.2 (§5, §6), bukan dari selera:
  - metrik primer anchor_bpb; uji konfirmatori di generasi terakhir, m = 3, kuantil-t df = n0-1
  - pemantauan berurutan m = 90 (normal), hanya untuk keputusan berhenti, bukan klaim
  - enam sinyal cedera dalam empat kelompok; keparahan dihitung per kelompok
  - aturan sadar-lantai: metrik di lantai/uninformative DIKELUARKAN, bukan dianggap "tidak cedera"
  - hasil tiga-nilai + uji ekuivalensi agar "tidak terdeteksi" tidak menjadi "aman"

Berkas ini tidak pernah membuka berkas kunci blinding. Ia memeriksa kode sumbernya sendiri dan
menolak berjalan bila menemukan rujukan ke berkas kunci.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import inspect
import json
import math
import re
import statistics
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from analysis.stats_r8 import t_ppf  # noqa: E402

P1_METRICS = ROOT / "runs" / "p1" / "metrics.jsonl"
P0_METRICS = ROOT / "runs" / "p0" / "metrics.jsonl"
BLIND_COMMIT = ROOT / "ops" / "blind_commitment.json"
OUT_MD = ROOT / "analysis" / "p1_conclusions.md"

ALPHA = 0.05
FLOOR_BPB = 0.03
M_CONFIRM = 3
M_MONITOR = 90
MIN_ARITH = 40
MIN_DOCS = 100

# kelompok cedera: nama -> daftar sinyal (PREREG §6)
GROUPS = {
    "G1_struktur": ["F1_degenerate", "F2_truncated"],
    "G2_keragaman": ["F3_ttr", "F4_near_dup"],
    "G3_distribusi": ["F5_entropi"],
    "G4_kapabilitas": ["F6_arith"],
}


def sha256_file(p: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def load_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    return [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pemeriksaan
def self_check() -> None:
    """Pastikan kode ini tidak pernah menyentuh berkas kunci blinding."""
    needle = ".blind" + "/" + "key" + ".json"
    src = inspect.getsource(sys.modules[__name__])
    code = re.sub(r'""".*?"""', "", src, flags=re.S)      # buang docstring
    code = "\n".join(line.split("#")[0] for line in code.splitlines())  # buang komentar
    if needle in code:
        raise SystemExit("DITOLAK: berkas analisis merujuk berkas kunci buta. "
                         "Analisis harus berjalan tanpa akses ke pemetaan arm.")


def check_inputs(path: Path) -> dict:
    rows = load_rows(path)
    base = [r for r in rows if str(r.get("arm")) in ("baseline", "M0", "base")]
    chains = [r for r in rows if r not in base]
    labels = sorted({str(r.get("arm")) for r in chains})
    need_eval = ["anchor_bpb", "arith_acc", "arith_n", "degenerate_rate", "truncated_rate",
                 "ttr", "near_dup_rate", "answer_entropy"]
    missing = {}
    for key in need_eval:
        absent = [r["model"] for r in rows if key not in (r.get("eval") or {})]
        if absent:
            missing[key] = len(absent)
    arith_n = min([(r.get("eval") or {}).get("arith_n", 0) for r in rows if r.get("eval")] or [0])
    return {
        "baris_total": len(rows),
        "baseline": len(base),
        "rantai": len(chains),
        "label_arm": labels,
        "label_buta": all(lbl.startswith("X") for lbl in labels) if labels else False,
        "field_hilang": missing,
        "arith_n_min": arith_n,
        "komitmen_buta": json.loads(BLIND_COMMIT.read_text()) if BLIND_COMMIT.exists() else None,
        "cukup_untuk_R8": (
            len(base) >= 5 and not missing and arith_n >= MIN_ARITH
        ),
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ statistik
def threshold_confirm(sigma0: float, n0: int) -> float:
    q = t_ppf(1 - ALPHA / (2 * M_CONFIRM), n0 - 1)
    return max(q * sigma0 * math.sqrt(1 + 1 / n0), FLOOR_BPB)


def threshold_monitor(sigma0: float, n0: int) -> float:
    from statistics import NormalDist
    q = NormalDist().inv_cdf(1 - ALPHA / (2 * M_MONITOR))
    return max(q * sigma0 * math.sqrt(1 + 1 / n0), FLOOR_BPB)


def mde80(sigma0: float, n0: int) -> float:
    q = t_ppf(1 - ALPHA / (2 * M_CONFIRM), n0 - 1)
    return (q + 0.8416212335729143) * sigma0 * math.sqrt(1 + 1 / n0)


def baseline_stats(base: list[dict]) -> dict:
    bpbs = [r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in base]
    mu0 = statistics.mean(bpbs)
    sigma0 = statistics.stdev(bpbs) if len(bpbs) > 1 else 0.0
    metrics: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for key in ("degenerate_rate", "truncated_rate", "ttr", "near_dup_rate", "answer_entropy", "arith_acc"):
        vals = [r["eval"].get(key) for r in base if r["eval"].get(key) is not None]
        if not vals:
            metrics[key] = {"tersedia": False}
            continue
        m = statistics.mean(vals)
        sd = statistics.stdev(vals) if len(vals) > 1 else 0.0
        n_items = (base[0]["eval"].get("arith_n") if key == "arith_acc" else MIN_DOCS) or MIN_DOCS
        at_floor = key == "arith_acc" and m <= 2 / max(1, n_items)
        uninformative = sd >= 0.5 * abs(m) if m else True
        metrics[key] = {
            "tersedia": True, "mean": round(m, 5), "sd": round(sd, 5),
            "label": "at_floor" if at_floor else ("uninformative" if uninformative else "informative"),
        }
    return {
        "n0": len(base), "mu0": mu0, "sigma0": sigma0,
        "se_pred": sigma0 * math.sqrt(1 + 1 / len(base)),
        "thr_confirm": threshold_confirm(sigma0, len(base)),
        "thr_monitor": threshold_monitor(sigma0, len(base)),
        "mde80": mde80(sigma0, len(base)),
        "delta_eq": max(FLOOR_BPB, 0.05 * mu0),
        "metrik": metrics,
    }


def harm_signals(row: dict, base: dict) -> dict[str, bool]:
    ev = row.get("eval", {})
    bm = base["metrik"]
    f1 = f2 = f3 = f4 = f5 = f6 = False
    if bm.get("degenerate_rate", {}).get("label") == "informative":
        f1 = (ev.get("degenerate_rate") or 0) - bm["degenerate_rate"]["mean"] >= 0.15
    if bm.get("truncated_rate", {}).get("label") == "informative":
        f2 = (ev.get("truncated_rate") or 0) - bm["truncated_rate"]["mean"] >= 0.20
    if bm.get("ttr", {}).get("label") == "informative":
        ref = bm["ttr"]["mean"]
        val = ev.get("ttr") or ref
        f3 = (val <= ref * 0.80) or (val <= ref - 3 * bm["ttr"]["sd"])
    if bm.get("near_dup_rate", {}).get("label") == "informative":
        f4 = (ev.get("near_dup_rate") or 0) - bm["near_dup_rate"]["mean"] >= 0.05
    if bm.get("answer_entropy", {}).get("label") == "informative":
        ref = bm["answer_entropy"]["mean"]
        f5 = (ev.get("answer_entropy") or ref) <= ref * 0.70
    if bm.get("arith_acc", {}).get("label") == "informative":
        f6 = bm["arith_acc"]["mean"] - (ev.get("arith_acc") or 0) >= 0.10
    signals = {"F1_degenerate": f1, "F2_truncated": f2, "F3_ttr": f3,
               "F4_near_dup": f4, "F5_entropi": f5, "F6_arith": f6}
    groups = {g: any(signals[s] for s in sigs if s in signals) for g, sigs in GROUPS.items()}
    SIG_TO_METRIC = {
        "F1_degenerate": "degenerate_rate", "F2_truncated": "truncated_rate",
        "F3_ttr": "ttr", "F4_near_dup": "near_dup_rate",
        "F5_entropi": "answer_entropy", "F6_arith": "arith_acc",
    }
    informative_groups = {
        g: any((bm.get(SIG_TO_METRIC[sig]) or {}).get("label") == "informative" for sig in sigs)
        for g, sigs in GROUPS.items()
    }
    return {"sinyal": signals, "kelompok": groups,
            "kelompok_informatif": informative_groups,
            "jumlah_informatif": sum(1 for v in informative_groups.values() if v)}


def decide(row: dict, base: dict) -> dict:
    bpb = row["eval"]["anchor_bpb"]
    delta = bpb - base["mu0"]
    thr = base["thr_confirm"]
    se = base["se_pred"]
    d_eq = base["delta_eq"]
    tq = t_ppf(0.95, base["n0"] - 1)
    upper = delta + tq * se           # CI satu-sisi 95% untuk Delta
    harm = harm_signals(row, base)
    groups_hit = sum(1 for v in harm["kelompok"].values() if v)

    if delta >= 2 * thr:
        need = 1
    elif delta >= thr:
        need = 2
    else:
        need = None

    if need is not None and harm["jumlah_informatif"] < 2:
        label = "inconclusive-harm"
    elif need is not None and groups_hit >= need:
        label = "collapse"
    elif upper < d_eq:
        label = "stabil"
    elif delta >= thr:
        label = "watch"
    else:
        label = "inconclusive"
    return {
        "bpb": bpb, "delta": delta, "thr": thr, "ci_upper": upper, "delta_eq": d_eq,
        "label": label, "kelompok_menyala": groups_hit, "kelompok_informatif": harm["jumlah_informatif"],
        "sinyal": harm["sinyal"], "perlu_kelompok": need,
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ agregasi seed
def aggregate(rs: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Rata-ratakan baris dari seed berbeda pada (label, generasi) yang sama.

    Tanpa ini, `decide()` akan memilih SATU baris seed secara sembarang dan membuang
    4/5 data — padahal 5 seed ada justru untuk meredam derau. Cacat ini ditemukan
    sebelum ada satu pun model rantai dilatih (2026-11-01) dan diperbaiki di sini.
    """
    ev: dict[str, float] = {}
    for k in ("anchor_bpb", "arith_acc", "degenerate_rate", "truncated_rate", "ttr",
              "near_dup_rate", "answer_entropy", "distinct2", "repetition_rate", "mean_bytes"):
        vals = [r["eval"][k] for r in rs if isinstance((r.get("eval") or {}).get(k), (int, float))]
        if vals:
            ev[k] = round(statistics.mean(vals), 6)
    ev["arith_n"] = min((r["eval"].get("arith_n") or 0) for r in rs)
    sd = statistics.stdev([r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in rs]) if len(rs) > 1 else 0.0
    return {"arm": rs[0]["arm"], "gen": rs[0].get("gen"), "model": f"rata-rata {len(rs)} seed",
            "eval": ev, "seed": None, "sd_bpb": round(sd, 6), "n_seed": len(rs)}


def by_generation(rows: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict[int, dict]]:
    groups: dict[tuple, list[dict]] = {}
    for r in rows:
        groups.setdefault((str(r["arm"]), int(r.get("gen") or 0)), []).append(r)
    out: dict[str, dict[int, dict]] = {}
    for (lbl, gen), rs in groups.items():
        out.setdefault(lbl, {})[gen] = aggregate(rs)
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ keluaran
def analyse(rows: list[dict], title: str, source: str) -> str:
    base_rows = [r for r in rows if str(r.get("arm")) in ("baseline", "M0", "base")]
    chains = [r for r in rows if r not in base_rows]
    base = baseline_stats(base_rows)
    by_label: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for r in chains:
        by_label.setdefault(str(r["arm"]), []).append(r)

    L = [f"# {title}", "",
         f"Sumber data: `{source}` · {len(base_rows)} model baseline, {len(chains)} model rantai, "
         f"{len(by_label)} label arm.", "",
         "> Hasil ini dihitung oleh `analysis/p1_analyze.py` dari metrik mentah. Label arm masih buta; "
         "pemetaan tidak dibuka oleh skrip ini.", "",
         "## Baseline", "",
         f"- n0 = {base['n0']} · mu0 = {base['mu0']:.4f} bpb · sigma0 = {base['sigma0']:.4f} · "
         f"SE_pred = {base['se_pred']:.4f}",
         f"- ambang konfirmatori (t, m=3) = **{base['thr_confirm']:.4f} bpb** · "
         f"ambang pemantauan (z, m=90) = {base['thr_monitor']:.4f} · MDE80 = {base['mde80']:.4f}",
         f"- delta_eq = {base['delta_eq']:.4f} bpb", "",
         "| Metrik sekunder | mean baseline | sd | label |", "| --- | --- | --- | --- |"]
    for k, v in base["metrik"].items():
        if v.get("tersedia"):
            L.append(f"| `{k}` | {v['mean']:.4f} | {v['sd']:.4f} | `{v['label']}` |")
        else:
            L.append(f"| `{k}` | – | – | `tidak tersedia` |")
    agg = by_generation(chains)
    L += ["", "## Keputusan per arm (generasi terakhir tiap rantai, dirata-ratakan antar-seed)", "",
          "| label | n | bpb akhir | Delta | CI95 atas | label keputusan | kelompok cedera |",
          "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |"]
    for lbl, gens in sorted(agg.items()):
        last = gens[max(gens)]
        d = decide(last, base)
        L.append(f"| {lbl} | {last['n_seed']} | {d['bpb']:.4f} | {d['delta']:+.4f} | {d['ci_upper']:+.4f} | "
                 f"**{d['label']}** | {d['kelompok_menyala']}/{d['kelompok_informatif']} |")
    L += ["", "## Pemantauan berurutan (screening, bukan klaim; rata-rata antar-seed)", "",
          "| label | gen | bpb | sd antar-seed | Delta | ambang pantau | lewat? |",
          "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |"]
    for lbl, gens in sorted(agg.items()):
        for gen in sorted(gens):
            r = gens[gen]
            delta = r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] - base["mu0"]
            L.append(f"| {lbl} | {gen} | {r['eval']['anchor_bpb']:.4f} | {r['sd_bpb']:.4f} | {delta:+.4f} | "
                     f"{base['thr_monitor']:.4f} | {'ya' if delta >= base['thr_monitor'] else 'tidak'} |")
    L += ["", "## Batas tafsir", "",
          "- `inconclusive` berarti data tidak memisahkan collapse dari stabil; itu bukan hasil negatif.",
          "- `watch` berarti ambang terlewati tanpa kelompok cedera terukur; itu bukan collapse.",
          "- Metrik berlabel `at_floor`/`uninformative` dikeluarkan dari corroboration dan tidak "
          "boleh dibaca sebagai ketiadaan cedera (PREREG §6.1).",
          "- Keputusan dihitung dari rata-rata antar-seed pada (label, generasi); sd antar-seed dilaporkan",
          "  terpisah agar penyebaran tidak tersembunyi di balik rata-rata.",
          "- Tren antar-generasi bersifat deskriptif; P1 tidak berdaya untuk uji tren inferensial."]
    return "\n".join(L) + "\n"


def main() -> int:
    self_check()
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    g.add_argument("--check", action="store_true")
    g.add_argument("--fixture", action="store_true")
    g.add_argument("--run", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.check:
        info = check_inputs(P1_METRICS)
        print(json.dumps(info, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        ok = info["cukup_untuk_R8"] and info["label_buta"]
        print("\nSIAP" if ok else "\nBELUM SIAP")
        return 0 if ok else 1

    if args.fixture:
        rows = load_rows(P0_METRICS)
        if not rows:
            print("GAGAL: runs/p0/metrics.jsonl tidak ada — jalankan pilot P0 dulu")
            return 1
        # fixture: data P0 tidak punya semua metrik R8; yang diuji adalah mesin statistiknya
        text = analyse(rows, "UJI UNIT mesin analisis pada data P0 (bukan hasil P1)",
                       "runs/p0/metrics.jsonl")
        out = Path("/tmp/p1_analyze_fixture.md")
        out.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        print(text[:1200])
        print(f"\nOK (fixture) -> {out}  sha256={sha256_file(out)[:16]}…")
        return 0

    rows = load_rows(P1_METRICS)
    if not rows:
        print("GAGAL: runs/p1/metrics.jsonl belum ada. P1 belum dijalankan.")
        return 1
    text = analyse(rows, "Kesimpulan P1 (label buta)", "runs/p1/metrics.jsonl")
    OUT_MD.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    digest = sha256_file(OUT_MD)
    print(f"OK -> {OUT_MD}")
    print(f"sha256 kesimpulan = {digest}")
    print("Langkah berikutnya (PREREG §8): ops/blind.py reveal --conclusions-sha256 " + digest)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
analysis/stats_r8.py · 4.8 KB · sha256 6275b22ed491…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Statistik untuk aturan R8 (pra-registrasi P1).

Murni pustaka standar: distribusi t Student lewat fungsi beta tak-lengkap,
dipakai untuk menetapkan ambang, MDE, dan batas ekuivalensi SEBELUM data P1 ada.

Semua angka yang dipakai PREREGISTRATION.md dihasilkan oleh `--table`,
sehingga dokumen pra-registrasi tidak memuat angka yang diketik tangan.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import math

FLOOR_BPB = 0.03          # lantai kepraktisan (bpb absolut)
ALPHA = 0.05              # family-wise
POWER = 0.80
QUANTILE_80 = 0.8416212335729143  # z_0.80


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- beta & t
def _betacf(a: float, b: float, x: float) -> float:
    tiny = 1e-30
    qab, qap, qam = a + b, a + 1.0, a - 1.0
    c = 1.0
    d = 1.0 - qab * x / qap
    if abs(d) < tiny:
        d = tiny
    d = 1.0 / d
    h = d
    for m in range(1, 300):
        m2 = 2 * m
        aa = m * (b - m) * x / ((qam + m2) * (a + m2))
        d = 1.0 + aa * d
        if abs(d) < tiny:
            d = tiny
        c = 1.0 + aa / c
        if abs(c) < tiny:
            c = tiny
        d = 1.0 / d
        h *= d * c
        aa = -(a + m) * (qab + m) * x / ((a + m2) * (qap + m2))
        d = 1.0 + aa * d
        if abs(d) < tiny:
            d = tiny
        c = 1.0 + aa / c
        if abs(c) < tiny:
            c = tiny
        d = 1.0 / d
        delta = d * c
        h *= delta
        if abs(delta - 1.0) < 1e-14:
            break
    return h


def betai(a: float, b: float, x: float) -> float:
    """I_x(a,b) ternormalisasi."""
    if x <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    if x >= 1.0:
        return 1.0
    lbeta = math.lgamma(a + b) - math.lgamma(a) - math.lgamma(b)
    front = math.exp(lbeta + a * math.log(x) + b * math.log(1.0 - x))
    if x < (a + 1.0) / (a + b + 2.0):
        return front * _betacf(a, b, x) / a
    return 1.0 - front * _betacf(b, a, 1.0 - x) / b


def t_cdf(t: float, df: int) -> float:
    x = df / (df + t * t)
    p = 0.5 * betai(df / 2.0, 0.5, x)
    return 1.0 - p if t > 0 else p


def t_ppf(p: float, df: int) -> float:
    """Kuantil t Student via bisection (presisi 1e-10)."""
    lo, hi = -1e4, 1e4
    for _ in range(200):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        if t_cdf(mid, df) < p:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return (lo + hi) / 2


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- aturan R8
def se_predictive(sigma0: float, n0: int) -> float:
    """Galat baku prediktif satu model baru terhadap mean baseline n0 seed."""
    return sigma0 * math.sqrt(1.0 + 1.0 / n0)


def threshold(sigma0: float, n0: int, m: int, alpha: float = ALPHA,
              floor: float = FLOOR_BPB) -> float:
    """Ambang R8: kuantil-t Bonferroni x galat baku prediktif, dengan lantai."""
    q = t_ppf(1.0 - alpha / (2.0 * m), n0 - 1)
    return max(q * se_predictive(sigma0, n0), floor)


def threshold_z(sigma0: float, n0: int, m: int, alpha: float = ALPHA,
                floor: float = FLOOR_BPB) -> float:
    """Versi normal (dipakai untuk pemantauan berurutan, bukan klaim konfirmatori)."""
    from statistics import NormalDist
    q = NormalDist().inv_cdf(1.0 - alpha / (2.0 * m))
    return max(q * se_predictive(sigma0, n0), floor)


def mde80(sigma0: float, n0: int, m: int, alpha: float = ALPHA) -> float:
    q = t_ppf(1.0 - alpha / (2.0 * m), n0 - 1)
    return (q + QUANTILE_80) * se_predictive(sigma0, n0)


def equivalence_bound(mu0: float) -> float:
    """Batas ekuivalensi: 'stabil' hanya bila seluruh CI di bawah batas ini."""
    return max(FLOOR_BPB, 0.05 * mu0)


def floor_binding(sigma0: float, n0: int, m: int) -> bool:
    return t_ppf(1.0 - ALPHA / (2.0 * m), n0 - 1) * se_predictive(sigma0, n0) < FLOOR_BPB


def table() -> str:
    out = []
    out.append("| sigma0 (bpb) | SE_pred (n0=5) | ambang konfirmatori (m=3) | ambang monitoring (m=90) | MDE80 konfirmatori | `delta_eq` bila mu0=0,90 |")
    out.append("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")
    for s in (0.005, 0.010, 0.0126, 0.015, 0.020, 0.030, 0.040, 0.060):
        se = se_predictive(s, 5)
        tc = threshold(s, 5, 3)
        tm = threshold(s, 5, 90)
        md = mde80(s, 5, 3)
        out.append(f"| {s:.4f} | {se:.4f} | {tc:.4f} | {tm:.4f} | {md:.4f} | {equivalence_bound(0.90):.4f} |")
    return "\n".join(out)


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--table", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.table:
        print(table())
        return
    print("t_ppf(0.975,4) =", round(t_ppf(0.975, 4), 4), "(harus 2.7764)")
    print("t_ppf(0.95,4)  =", round(t_ppf(0.95, 4), 4), "(harus 2.1318)")
    print("t_ppf(0.999722,4) =", round(t_ppf(1 - 0.05 / (2 * 90), 4), 4))
    print("t_ppf(0.991667,4) =", round(t_ppf(1 - 0.05 / (2 * 3), 4), 4))
    print()
    print(table())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
analysis/t0_audit.py · 19.2 KB · sha256 7db141c503fd…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Audit gerbang korpus T0 untuk P1 (PREREGISTRATION.md v1.1 bagian 2.1).

Lima gerbang diperiksa apa adanya, ditambah tiga pembanding yang membuat hasilnya
bisa ditafsirkan:

  1. referensi manusia — 8-gram/kompresi dari teks yang jelas bukan buatan saya
     (docstring pustaka standar Python), dipotong pada ukuran yang sama;
  2. korpus P0 — untuk menunjukkan apakah perbaikan ini nyata atau hanya klaim;
  3. entropi silang Markov orde-3 pada test split — proksi murah untuk
     "apakah korpus ini bisa dihafal" tanpa melatih satu model pun.

Pemakaian:
    python3 analysis/t0_audit.py            # cetak ringkasan + tulis laporan
    python3 analysis/t0_audit.py --json     # keluaran mesin saja
Keluar dengan kode 1 bila ada gerbang yang gagal.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import glob
import json
import math
import random
import re
import sys
import zlib
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
T0 = ROOT / "data" / "p1" / "t0"
OUT_JSON = ROOT / "runs" / "p1" / "t0_audit.json"
OUT_MD = ROOT / "analysis" / "t0_audit_report.md"

# --- jangkar yang dibekukan di pra-registrasi v1.2 §2.1a -------------------
P0_REF = {"uniq8": 0.1870, "hapax8": 0.1223, "zlib_bpb": 1.189, "ttr": 0.0732, "markov_ce": 1.615}
HUMAN_ZLIB_2MB = 2.217
T2_MULT = {"uniq8": 1.50, "hapax8": 1.50, "zlib_bpb": 1.50, "ttr": 1.40, "markov_ce": 1.35}
T2 = {
    "T2a": ("uniq-8 potongan 156.697 B", P0_REF["uniq8"] * T2_MULT["uniq8"]),
    "T2b": ("hapax-8 potongan 156.697 B", P0_REF["hapax8"] * T2_MULT["hapax8"]),
    "T2c": ("zlib bpb seluruh isi (juga >= 0,85 x manusia)", max(P0_REF["zlib_bpb"] * T2_MULT["zlib_bpb"],
                                                               0.85 * HUMAN_ZLIB_2MB)),
    "T2d": ("TTR sampel 200 KB", P0_REF["ttr"] * T2_MULT["ttr"]),
    "T2e": ("entropi Markov orde-3 @156.697 B", P0_REF["markov_ce"] * T2_MULT["markov_ce"]),
}

GATES = {
    "T1": "ukuran >= 2.097.152 byte",
    "T2": "kriteria terkalibrasi v1.2: T2a-T2e (uniq-8, hapax, zlib, TTR, entropi Markov)",
    "T3": ">= 12 domain, tiap domain >= 5% byte",
    "T4": "median panjang 150-600 B, p1 >= 80 B, p99 <= 2.000 B",
    "T5": "Jaccard 5-gram antar-dokumen maksimum <= 0,5",
}


def load_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    return [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]


def uniq_ngram(text: str, n: int = 8) -> float:
    grams = [text[i:i + n] for i in range(len(text) - n + 1)]
    return len(set(grams)) / max(1, len(grams))


def hapax_share(text: str, n: int = 8) -> float:
    c = Counter(text[i:i + n] for i in range(len(text) - n + 1))
    return sum(1 for v in c.values() if v == 1) / max(1, sum(c.values()))


def zlib_bpb(text: str) -> float:
    raw = text.encode("utf-8")
    return len(zlib.compress(raw, 9)) * 8 / max(1, len(raw))


def ttr(text: str, limit: int = 200_000) -> float:
    w = text[:limit].lower().split()
    return len(set(w)) / max(1, len(w))


def quantile(sorted_vals: list[int], p: float) -> int:
    if not sorted_vals:
        return 0
    return sorted_vals[min(len(sorted_vals) - 1, int(p * (len(sorted_vals) - 1)))]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ referensi
def human_reference(n_bytes: int, seed: int = 7) -> dict:
    """Docstring pustaka standar Python — teks yang tidak saya tulis."""
    docs: list[str] = []
    for f in sorted(glob.glob("/usr/lib/python3.11/**/*.py", recursive=True)):
        try:
            t = Path(f).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        except OSError:
            continue
        docs.append(" ".join(re.findall(r'"""(..*?)"""', t, flags=re.S)))
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    rng.shuffle(docs)  # campur antar-berkas agar tidak satu gaya
    text = " ".join(docs)[:n_bytes]
    return {
        "sumber": "docstring pustaka standar Python 3.11 (ditulis manusia)",
        "bytes": len(text.encode()),
        "uniq8": round(uniq_ngram(text), 4),
        "hapax8": round(hapax_share(text), 4),
        "zlib_bpb": round(zlib_bpb(text), 3),
        "ttr": round(ttr(text), 4),
    }


def markov_ce_split(text: str, frac: float = 0.8, order: int = 3) -> float:
    cut = int(len(text) * frac)
    return markov_cross_entropy(text[:cut], text[cut:], order=order)


def p0_corpus_reference() -> dict:
    """Korpus P0 (templat 200 KB) sebagai pembanding 'seburuk apa yang mungkin'."""
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
    from src.corpus import build_teacher_corpus  # noqa: PLC0415
    docs = build_teacher_corpus(200_000, 13)
    content = " ".join(d.answer for d in docs) if hasattr(docs[0], "answer") else \
        " ".join(re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", d.text) for d in docs)
    return {
        "sumber": "korpus P0 (templat, 6 domain)",
        "docs": len(docs),
        "bytes": len(content.encode()),
        "uniq8": round(uniq_ngram(content), 4),
        "hapax8": round(hapax_share(content), 4),
        "zlib_bpb": round(zlib_bpb(content), 3),
        "ttr": round(ttr(content), 4),
        "markov_ce": round(markov_ce_split(content), 4),
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ proksi keterhafalan
def markov_cross_entropy(train: str, test: str, order: int = 3, alpha: float = 0.25) -> float:
    """Bits/byte Markov orde-3 dengan penghalusan add-alpha. Tanpa pelatihan gradien."""
    counts: dict[str, Counter] = defaultdict(Counter)
    totals: Counter = Counter()
    for i in range(len(train) - order):
        ctx, nxt = train[i:i + order], train[i + order]
        counts[ctx][nxt] += 1
        totals[ctx] += 1
    vocab = 256
    bits = 0.0
    for i in range(len(test) - order):
        ctx, nxt = test[i:i + order], test[i + order]
        c = counts.get(ctx)
        if c:
            p = (c[nxt] + alpha) / (totals[ctx] + alpha * vocab)
        else:
            p = 1.0 / vocab
        bits += -math.log2(p)
    return bits / max(1, len(test) - order)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ gerbang
def jaccard_max(docs: list[dict], n: int = 5, sample: int = 300, seed: int = 11) -> tuple[float, str]:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    sel = rng.sample(docs, min(sample, len(docs)))
    grams = []
    for d in sel:
        w = d["answer"].lower().split()
        grams.append({tuple(w[i:i + n]) for i in range(max(0, len(w) - n + 1))})
    worst, pair = 0.0, ""
    for i in range(len(grams)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(grams)):
            a, b = grams[i], grams[j]
            if not a or not b:
                continue
            inter = len(a & b)
            if not inter:
                continue
            jac = inter / len(a | b)
            if jac > worst:
                worst, pair = jac, f"{sel[i]['doc_id']} vs {sel[j]['doc_id']}"
    return worst, pair


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    manifest = json.loads((T0 / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    train = load_jsonl(T0 / "train.jsonl")
    valid = load_jsonl(T0 / "valid.jsonl")
    test = load_jsonl(T0 / "test.jsonl")
    anchor = load_jsonl(T0 / "anchor.jsonl")
    probes = load_jsonl(T0 / "arith_probes.jsonl")
    if not train:
        print("GAGAL: data/p1/t0/train.jsonl kosong — jalankan python -m src.p1_corpus --build")
        return 1

    all_docs = train + valid + test
    content = " ".join(d["answer"] for d in all_docs)
    markup = "".join(d["text"] for d in all_docs)
    lens = sorted(d["n_bytes"] for d in all_docs)
    per_domain: Counter = Counter()
    for d in all_docs:
        per_domain[d["domain"]] += d["n_bytes"]
    total_bytes = sum(per_domain.values())
    shares = {k: v / total_bytes for k, v in per_domain.items()}

    results: dict[str, dict] = {}
    results["T1"] = {
        "lulus": total_bytes >= 2_097_152,
        "nilai": total_bytes,
        "syarat": ">= 2097152",
    }
    u8 = uniq_ngram(content)
    results["T3"] = {
        "lulus": len(shares) >= 12 and min(shares.values()) >= 0.05,
        "nilai": {"domain": len(shares), "porsi_terkecil": round(min(shares.values()), 4)},
        "syarat": ">= 12 domain dan tiap domain >= 5%",
        "porsi": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in sorted(shares.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])},
    }
    med = quantile(lens, 0.5)
    results["T4"] = {
        "lulus": 150 <= med <= 600 and quantile(lens, 0.01) >= 80 and quantile(lens, 0.99) <= 2000,
        "nilai": {"median": med, "p1": quantile(lens, 0.01), "p99": quantile(lens, 0.99)},
        "syarat": "median 150-600, p1 >= 80, p99 <= 2000",
    }
    jac, pair = jaccard_max(all_docs)
    results["T5"] = {"lulus": jac <= 0.5, "nilai": round(jac, 4), "syarat": "<= 0,5", "pasangan": pair}

    # pembanding
    human = human_reference(len(content.encode()))
    human_small = human_reference(156_697)  # ukuran sebanding korpus P0
    p0ref = p0_corpus_reference()
    # potongan acak seukuran korpus P0: perbandingan setara bahasa DAN ukuran
    rng = random.Random(3)
    chunks, pos = [], 0
    while sum(len(c) for c in chunks) < 156_697 and pos < len(content) - 1000:
        n = 600 + rng.randint(0, 600)
        chunks.append(content[pos:pos + n])
        pos += n + rng.randint(0, 400)
    p1_small = " ".join(chunks)[:156_697]

    ce_train = "".join(d["text"] for d in train)
    ce_test = "".join(d["text"] for d in test)
    ce = markov_cross_entropy(ce_train, ce_test)

    diag = {
        "pada_ukuran_p0": {
            "uniq8": round(uniq_ngram(p1_small), 4),
            "hapax8": round(hapax_share(p1_small), 4),
            "zlib_bpb": round(zlib_bpb(p1_small), 3),
            "ttr": round(ttr(p1_small), 4),
            "markov_ce": round(markov_ce_split(p1_small), 4),
        },
        "konten_byte": len(content.encode()),
        "hapax8": round(hapax_share(content), 4),
        "zlib_bpb": round(zlib_bpb(content), 3),
        "ttr": round(ttr(content), 4),
        "markov_ce_bpb_test": round(ce, 4),
        "docs": len(all_docs),
        "anchor_docs": len(anchor),
        "arith_probes": len(probes),
        "probe_contoh": probes[0] if probes else None,
        "split_byte": manifest["splits"],
    }
    # --- T2 v1.2: lima sub-kriteria terkalibrasi (PREREGISTRATION.md §2.1a) ---
    ps = diag["pada_ukuran_p0"]
    sub = {
        "T2a": round(ps["uniq8"], 4),
        "T2b": round(ps["hapax8"], 4),
        "T2c": round(diag["zlib_bpb"], 3),
        "T2d": round(diag["ttr"], 4),
        "T2e": round(ps["markov_ce"], 4),
    }
    results["T2"] = {
        "lulus": all(sub[k] >= T2[k][1] for k in T2),
        "nilai": sub,
        "syarat": "T2a-T2e (lihat porsi di bawah)",
        "sub": {
            k: {"metrik": T2[k][0], "ambang": round(T2[k][1], 4), "nilai": sub[k], "lulus": sub[k] >= T2[k][1]}
            for k in T2
        },
        "versi_lama_v1_1": {"syarat": ">= 0,98", "nilai": round(u8, 4), "lulus": u8 >= 0.98},
        "catatan": "T2 v1.1 absolut mustahil; diganti v1.2 terkalibrasi, diratifikasi 2026-11-01",
    }

    failed = [k for k, v in results.items() if not v["lulus"]]
    payload = {
        "gates": GATES,
        "results": results,
        "diagnostik": diag,
        "referensi_manusia": human,
        "referensi_manusia_pada_ukuran_p0": human_small,
        "referensi_p0": p0ref,
        "gagal": failed,
        "lulus_semua": not failed,
    }
    OUT_JSON.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    OUT_JSON.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0 if not failed else 1

    # ---------- laporan markdown ----------
    L = []
    L.append("# Audit korpus T0 (P1)")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"**Hasil: {'SEMUA GERBANG LULUS' if not failed else 'GAGAL pada ' + ', '.join(failed)}**")
    L.append("")
    L.append("| Gerbang | Syarat | Nilai | Hasil |")
    L.append("| --- | --- | --- | --- |")
    for k, v in results.items():
        val = v["nilai"] if not isinstance(v["nilai"], dict) else json.dumps(v["nilai"], ensure_ascii=False)
        L.append(f"| {k} | {v['syarat']} | `{val}` | {'LULUS' if v['lulus'] else '**GAGAL**'} |")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Rincian T2 v1.2 (terkalibrasi, PREREGISTRATION.md §2.1a)")
    L.append("")
    L.append("| Kode | Metrik | Ambang | Nilai | Hasil |")
    L.append("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")
    for k, v in results["T2"]["sub"].items():
        L.append(f"| {k} | {v['metrik']} | {v['ambang']} | {v['nilai']} | {'LULUS' if v['lulus'] else '**GAGAL**'} |")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"Versi lama (v1.1, absolut >= 0,98): nilai {results['T2']['versi_lama_v1_1']['nilai']} -> "
             "tetap GAGAL, dan tetap dicatat sebagai riwayat. Syarat itu mustahil dicapai korpus bahasa apa pun.")
    L.append("")
    L.append("## Pembanding yang membuat angka di atas bisa ditafsirkan")
    L.append("")
    L.append("| Sumber | byte | uniq-8 | hapax-8 | zlib bpb | TTR |")
    L.append("") if False else None
    L.append("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")
    L.append(f"| Korpus P1 (isi `<A>`) | {diag['konten_byte']} | {u8:.4f} | {diag['hapax8']:.4f} | "
             f"{diag['zlib_bpb']:.3f} | {diag['ttr']:.4f} |")
    L.append(f"| Korpus P0 (templat, 156 KB) | {p0ref['bytes']} | {p0ref['uniq8']:.4f}* | {p0ref['hapax8']:.4f}* | "
             f"{p0ref['zlib_bpb']:.3f} | {p0ref['ttr']:.4f} |")
    L.append(f"| Manusia pada 156 KB (sebanding P0) | {human_small['bytes']} | {human_small['uniq8']:.4f}* | "
             f"{human_small['hapax8']:.4f}* | {human_small['zlib_bpb']:.3f} | {human_small['ttr']:.4f} |")
    L.append(f"| **Manusia** (docstring stdlib, ukuran sama) | {human['bytes']} | {human['uniq8']:.4f} | "
             f"{human['hapax8']:.4f} | {human['zlib_bpb']:.3f} | {human['ttr']:.4f} |")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"- Entropi silang Markov orde-3 pada test split: **{ce:.3f} bits/byte** "
             "(proksi keterhafalan; makin rendah makin mudah dihafal).")
    L.append("* uniq-8 dan hapax **bergantung ukuran**: pada 156 KB nilainya jauh lebih tinggi "
             f"daripada pada 2 MB ({human_small['uniq8']:.4f} vs {human['uniq8']:.4f} untuk teks manusia "
             "yang sama). Karena itu keduanya hanya boleh dibandingkan pada ukuran yang sama; P0 (156 KB) "
             "tidak boleh dibandingkan langsung dengan P1 (2 MB).")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"- Entropi silang Markov P0: **{p0ref['markov_ce']:.3f} bits/byte** "
             f"(P1: {ce:.3f}) — metrik ini tidak bergantung ukuran corpus.")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"- Rasio diversitas P1/manusia: uniq-8 **{u8 / human['uniq8']:.2f}x**, "
             f"hapax **{diag['hapax8'] / human['hapax8']:.2f}x**, zlib **{diag['zlib_bpb'] / human['zlib_bpb']:.2f}x**, "
             f"TTR **{diag['ttr'] / human['ttr']:.2f}x**.")
    L.append("")
    L.append("## Kenapa T2 gagal, dan kenapa itu bukan alasan menurunkan gerbang")
    L.append("")
    L.append("T2 meminta 98% 8-gram unik. Teks manusia pada ukuran yang sama hanya mencapai "
             f"**{human['uniq8']:.2f}**. Pada 2 MB, bahasa alami selalu mengulang frasa pendek "
             "(\" selama \", \" yang \", nama berulang), sehingga porsi 8-gram unik **tidak mungkin** "
             "mendekati 0,98 untuk korpus apa pun yang masih terbaca sebagai bahasa. Gerbang ini "
             "dirumuskan tanpa kalibrasi, dan P0 tidak pernah mengujinya karena belum ada korpus P1.")
    L.append("")
    L.append("Keputusan yang saya ambil: **gerbang tidak saya turunkan sendiri**. Usulan amandemen "
             "v1.2 ada di berkas ini bagian bawah, menunggu ratifikasi manusia. Sampai diratifikasi, "
             "P1 tetap terkunci.")
    L.append("")
    L.append("## Status korpus")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"- {diag['docs']} dokumen, {diag['konten_byte']:,} byte isi, split "
             f"{ {k: v['docs'] for k, v in manifest['splits'].items()} }".replace(",", "."))
    L.append(f"- anchor test {diag['anchor_docs']} dokumen (tidak ikut training), "
             f"{diag['arith_probes']} probe aritmetika held-out")
    L.append(f"- domain: {json.dumps(results['T3']['porsi'], ensure_ascii=False)}")
    L.append("")
    L.append("## Usulan amandemen T2 (v1.2) — MENUNGGU RATIFIKASI")
    L.append("")
    L.append("Ganti T2 absolut dengan **kriteria fungsional** yang mengukur hal yang sebenarnya "
             "kita takutkan (korpus yang bisa dihafal sehingga baseline jatuh ke ekor seperti P0), "
             "ditambah lantai diversitas relatif terhadap manusia:")
    L.append("")
    L.append("| Usulan | Ambang (berlabuh pada P0 dan manusia) | Nilai P1 @156 KB | P0 @156 KB | Rasio |")
    L.append("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")
    ps = diag["pada_ukuran_p0"]
    L.append(f"| T2a uniq-8 (ukuran sama) | >= 1,50 x P0 = {1.5 * p0ref['uniq8']:.4f} | {ps['uniq8']:.4f} | "
             f"{p0ref['uniq8']:.4f} | {ps['uniq8'] / p0ref['uniq8']:.2f}x |")
    L.append(f"| T2b hapax-8 (ukuran sama) | >= 1,50 x P0 = {1.5 * p0ref['hapax8']:.4f} | {ps['hapax8']:.4f} | "
             f"{p0ref['hapax8']:.4f} | {ps['hapax8'] / p0ref['hapax8']:.2f}x |")
    L.append(f"| T2c zlib bpb (bebas ukuran) | >= 1,50 x P0 = {1.5 * p0ref['zlib_bpb']:.3f} **dan** >= 0,85 x manusia "
             f"({0.85 * human['zlib_bpb']:.3f}) | {ps['zlib_bpb']:.3f} | {p0ref['zlib_bpb']:.3f} | "
             f"{ps['zlib_bpb'] / p0ref['zlib_bpb']:.2f}x |")
    L.append(f"| T2d TTR sampel 200 KB (bebas ukuran) | >= 1,40 x P0 = {1.4 * p0ref['ttr']:.4f} | {ps['ttr']:.4f} | "
             f"{p0ref['ttr']:.4f} | {ps['ttr'] / p0ref['ttr']:.2f}x |")
    L.append(f"| T2e entropi Markov @156 KB (bebas ukuran) | >= 1,35 x P0 = {1.35 * p0ref['markov_ce']:.3f} | "
             f"{ps['markov_ce']:.3f} | {p0ref['markov_ce']:.3f} | {ps['markov_ce'] / p0ref['markov_ce']:.2f}x |")
    L.append("")
    L.append(f"Untuk konteks, teks manusia pada ukuran yang sama: uniq-8 {human_small['uniq8']:.4f}, "
             f"hapax {human_small['hapax8']:.4f}, zlib {human_small['zlib_bpb']:.3f}, CE {human_small['markov_ce'] if 'markov_ce' in human_small else float('nan'):.3f}. "
             f"Korpus P1 berada pada {ps['uniq8'] / human_small['uniq8']:.2f}x uniq-8 dan "
             f"{ps['markov_ce'] / 3.4724:.2f}x entropi manusia — lebih rendah, dan itu wajar karena bahasanya berbeda "
             "(Indonesia vs Inggris+kode), bukan karena algoritmanya sama.")
    L.append("")
    L.append("**Pengakuan bias:** kelipatan 1,50 / 1,40 / 1,35 saya tetapkan setelah melihat angka korpus "
             "ini dan angka P0. Itu tetap *threshold fitting*, hanya dengan dua jangkar eksternal "
             "(P0 sebagai lantai, teks manusia sebagai plafon) alih-alih satu angka yang dipilih agar lolos. "
             "Yang membuatnya bisa dipertanggungjawabkan: (a) ambang dinyatakan sekarang, sebelum satu model "
             "P1 pun dilatih; (b) arahnya tidak bisa dipakai untuk mempermudah klaim collapse — T2 adalah "
             "gerbang korpus, bukan gerbang hasil; (c) kalau kamu menolak, T2 tetap 0,98, P1 tidak berjalan, "
             "dan itu akan saya laporkan sebagai kegagalan pra-registrasi, bukan sebagai alasan mengubahnya diam-diam.")
    L.append("")
    OUT_MD.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    print("\n".join(L[:14]))
    print(f"\nJSON : {OUT_JSON}")
    print(f"LAPORAN: {OUT_MD}")
    return 0 if not failed else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# Audit korpus T0 (P1)

**Hasil: SEMUA GERBANG LULUS**

| Gerbang | Syarat | Nilai | Hasil |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T1 | >= 2097152 | `2301328` | LULUS |
| T3 | >= 12 domain dan tiap domain >= 5% | `{"domain": 14, "porsi_terkecil": 0.0504}` | LULUS |
| T4 | median 150-600, p1 >= 80, p99 <= 2000 | `{"median": 483, "p1": 221, "p99": 1752}` | LULUS |
| T5 | <= 0,5 | `0.1196` | LULUS |
| T2 | T2a-T2e (lihat porsi di bawah) | `{"T2a": 0.38, "T2b": 0.261, "T2c": 2.048, "T2d": 0.1134, "T2e": 2.6633}` | LULUS |

### Rincian T2 v1.2 (terkalibrasi, PREREGISTRATION.md §2.1a)

| Kode | Metrik | Ambang | Nilai | Hasil |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T2a | uniq-8 potongan 156.697 B | 0.2805 | 0.38 | LULUS |
| T2b | hapax-8 potongan 156.697 B | 0.1835 | 0.261 | LULUS |
| T2c | zlib bpb seluruh isi (juga >= 0,85 x manusia) | 1.8844 | 2.048 | LULUS |
| T2d | TTR sampel 200 KB | 0.1025 | 0.1134 | LULUS |
| T2e | entropi Markov orde-3 @156.697 B | 2.1803 | 2.6633 | LULUS |

Versi lama (v1.1, absolut >= 0,98): nilai 0.1529 -> tetap GAGAL, dan tetap dicatat sebagai riwayat. Syarat itu mustahil dicapai korpus bahasa apa pun.

## Pembanding yang membuat angka di atas bisa ditafsirkan

| Sumber | byte | uniq-8 | hapax-8 | zlib bpb | TTR |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Korpus P1 (isi `<A>`) | 2028237 | 0.1529 | 0.0921 | 2.048 | 0.1134 |
| Korpus P0 (templat, 156 KB) | 156697 | 0.1870* | 0.1223* | 1.189 | 0.0732 |
| Manusia pada 156 KB (sebanding P0) | 156701 | 0.5324* | 0.3886* | 2.486 | 0.2123 |
| **Manusia** (docstring stdlib, ukuran sama) | 2028760 | 0.3013 | 0.1895 | 2.216 | 0.1973 |

- Entropi silang Markov orde-3 pada test split: **1.785 bits/byte** (proksi keterhafalan; makin rendah makin mudah dihafal).
* uniq-8 dan hapax **bergantung ukuran**: pada 156 KB nilainya jauh lebih tinggi daripada pada 2 MB (0.5324 vs 0.3013 untuk teks manusia yang sama). Karena itu keduanya hanya boleh dibandingkan pada ukuran yang sama; P0 (156 KB) tidak boleh dibandingkan langsung dengan P1 (2 MB).

- Entropi silang Markov P0: **1.615 bits/byte** (P1: 1.785) — metrik ini tidak bergantung ukuran corpus.

- Rasio diversitas P1/manusia: uniq-8 **0.51x**, hapax **0.49x**, zlib **0.92x**, TTR **0.57x**.

## Kenapa T2 gagal, dan kenapa itu bukan alasan menurunkan gerbang

T2 meminta 98% 8-gram unik. Teks manusia pada ukuran yang sama hanya mencapai **0.30**. Pada 2 MB, bahasa alami selalu mengulang frasa pendek (" selama ", " yang ", nama berulang), sehingga porsi 8-gram unik **tidak mungkin** mendekati 0,98 untuk korpus apa pun yang masih terbaca sebagai bahasa. Gerbang ini dirumuskan tanpa kalibrasi, dan P0 tidak pernah mengujinya karena belum ada korpus P1.

Keputusan yang saya ambil: **gerbang tidak saya turunkan sendiri**. Usulan amandemen v1.2 ada di berkas ini bagian bawah, menunggu ratifikasi manusia. Sampai diratifikasi, P1 tetap terkunci.

## Status korpus

- 3165 dokumen. 2.028.237 byte isi. split {'train': 3057. 'valid': 59. 'test': 49}
- anchor test 153 dokumen (tidak ikut training), 60 probe aritmetika held-out
- domain: {"kode": 0.105, "perbandingan": 0.0756, "jurnal": 0.0745, "kebijakan": 0.073, "ringkasan": 0.0722, "narasi": 0.0722, "faktual": 0.0718, "sains": 0.0714, "tinjauan": 0.0709, "analisis": 0.0702, "definisi": 0.0688, "aritmetika": 0.0626, "dialog": 0.0614, "prosedur": 0.0504}

## Usulan amandemen T2 (v1.2) — MENUNGGU RATIFIKASI

Ganti T2 absolut dengan **kriteria fungsional** yang mengukur hal yang sebenarnya kita takutkan (korpus yang bisa dihafal sehingga baseline jatuh ke ekor seperti P0), ditambah lantai diversitas relatif terhadap manusia:

| Usulan | Ambang (berlabuh pada P0 dan manusia) | Nilai P1 @156 KB | P0 @156 KB | Rasio |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T2a uniq-8 (ukuran sama) | >= 1,50 x P0 = 0.2805 | 0.3800 | 0.1870 | 2.03x |
| T2b hapax-8 (ukuran sama) | >= 1,50 x P0 = 0.1835 | 0.2610 | 0.1223 | 2.13x |
| T2c zlib bpb (bebas ukuran) | >= 1,50 x P0 = 1.784 **dan** >= 0,85 x manusia (1.884) | 2.146 | 1.189 | 1.80x |
| T2d TTR sampel 200 KB (bebas ukuran) | >= 1,40 x P0 = 0.1025 | 0.1331 | 0.0732 | 1.82x |
| T2e entropi Markov @156 KB (bebas ukuran) | >= 1,35 x P0 = 2.181 | 2.663 | 1.615 | 1.65x |

Untuk konteks, teks manusia pada ukuran yang sama: uniq-8 0.5324, hapax 0.3886, zlib 2.486, CE nan. Korpus P1 berada pada 0.71x uniq-8 dan 0.77x entropi manusia — lebih rendah, dan itu wajar karena bahasanya berbeda (Indonesia vs Inggris+kode), bukan karena algoritmanya sama.

**Pengakuan bias:** kelipatan 1,50 / 1,40 / 1,35 saya tetapkan setelah melihat angka korpus ini dan angka P0. Itu tetap *threshold fitting*, hanya dengan dua jangkar eksternal (P0 sebagai lantai, teks manusia sebagai plafon) alih-alih satu angka yang dipilih agar lolos. Yang membuatnya bisa dipertanggungjawabkan: (a) ambang dinyatakan sekarang, sebelum satu model P1 pun dilatih; (b) arahnya tidak bisa dipakai untuk mempermudah klaim collapse — T2 adalah gerbang korpus, bukan gerbang hasil; (c) kalau kamu menolak, T2 tetap 0,98, P1 tidak berjalan, dan itu akan saya laporkan sebagai kegagalan pra-registrasi, bukan sebagai alasan mengubahnya diam-diam.

{

dashboard/assets/app.js · 4.8 KB · sha256 b45fdb40b90f…
/* Dashboard transparansi — interaksi minimum, tanpa dependensi. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  /* ---------- 1. sorot bagian aktif di sidebar ---------- */
  var links = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll("#nav a"));
  var sections = links
    .map(function (a) { return document.querySelector(a.getAttribute("href")); })
    .filter(Boolean);

  if ("IntersectionObserver" in window && sections.length) {
    var visible = new Map();
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(
      function (entries) {
        entries.forEach(function (e) { visible.set(e.target.id, e.isIntersecting ? e.intersectionRatio : 0); });
        var best = null, bestScore = -1;
        visible.forEach(function (score, id) {
          if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = id; }
        });
        links.forEach(function (a) {
          a.classList.toggle("active", a.getAttribute("href") === "#" + best);
        });
      },
      { rootMargin: "-84px 0px -55% 0px", threshold: [0, 0.15, 0.4, 0.8] }
    );
    sections.forEach(function (s) { io.observe(s); });
  }

  /* ---------- 2. salin perintah ---------- */
  var toast;
  function notify(msg) {
    if (!toast) {
      toast = document.createElement("div");
      toast.style.cssText =
        "position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:26px;transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(10px);" +
        "background:#12211d;color:#9fe6d2;border:1px solid #1d4d43;border-radius:8px;" +
        "padding:9px 16px;font:12px/1.4 ui-monospace,monospace;z-index:200;opacity:0;" +
        "transition:opacity .18s,transform .18s;pointer-events:none;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.45)";
      document.body.appendChild(toast);
    }
    toast.textContent = msg;
    toast.style.opacity = "1";
    toast.style.transform = "translateX(-50%) translateY(0)";
    clearTimeout(toast._t);
    toast._t = setTimeout(function () {
      toast.style.opacity = "0";
      toast.style.transform = "translateX(-50%) translateY(10px)";
    }, 1600);
  }

  function copy(text) {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
      return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(
        function () { notify("tersalin: " + text.slice(0, 60)); },
        function () { notify("gagal menyalin — pilih teksnya manual"); }
      );
    }
    var ta = document.createElement("textarea");
    ta.value = text;
    ta.style.position = "fixed";
    ta.style.opacity = "0";
    document.body.appendChild(ta);
    ta.select();
    try { document.execCommand("copy"); notify("tersalin: " + text.slice(0, 60)); }
    catch (err) { notify("gagal menyalin — pilih teksnya manual"); }
    document.body.removeChild(ta);
  }

  document.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
    var btn = ev.target.closest("button[data-copy]");
    if (btn) { copy(btn.getAttribute("data-copy")); return; }
    var cell = ev.target.closest("td.cmd");
    if (cell) { copy(cell.getAttribute("data-cmd") || cell.textContent.trim()); }
  });

  /* ---------- 3. pencarian cepat di inventaris berkas ---------- */
  var table = document.querySelector("#berkas table");
  if (table && table.tBodies.length) {
    var box = document.createElement("input");
    box.type = "search";
    box.placeholder = "Saring nama berkas…";
    box.setAttribute("aria-label", "Saring nama berkas");
    box.style.cssText =
      "width:100%;margin:6px 0 10px;padding:9px 13px;border-radius:8px;" +
      "border:1px solid #1f2c3a;background:#080d12;color:#e6edf3;" +
      "font:12.5px ui-monospace,monospace;outline:none";
    table.parentNode.insertBefore(box, table);

    box.addEventListener("input", function () {
      var q = box.value.toLowerCase();
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(table.tBodies[0].rows, function (row) {
        row.style.display = row.cells[0].textContent.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) > -1 ? "" : "none";
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 4. buka/tutup semua penampil berkas ---------- */
  var viewers = document.querySelectorAll("details.viewer");
  if (viewers.length > 2) {
    var tools = document.createElement("p");
    tools.className = "small";
    tools.innerHTML =
      '<button data-all="open" style="cursor:pointer;background:#121c26;color:#8fa1b6;' +
      'border:1px solid #1f2c3a;border-radius:6px;padding:4px 10px;font:11px ui-monospace,monospace">buka semua</button> ' +
      '<button data-all="close" style="cursor:pointer;background:#121c26;color:#8fa1b6;' +
      'border:1px solid #1f2c3a;border-radius:6px;padding:4px 10px;font:11px ui-monospace,monospace">tutup semua</button>';
    viewers[0].parentNode.insertBefore(tools, viewers[0]);
    tools.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
      var btn = ev.target.closest("button[data-all]");
      if (!btn) return;
      var open = btn.getAttribute("data-all") === "open";
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(viewers, function (d) { d.open = open; });
    });
  }
})();

dashboard/build.py · 22.9 KB · sha256 a75bc8d50aa5…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Bangun dashboard statis dari PLAN.md + status.json.

Sengaja tanpa dependensi eksternal (tanpa paket markdown, tanpa CDN) supaya
siapa pun bisa membangun ulang dashboard ini di mesin mana pun:

    python3 dashboard/build.py

Prinsip transparansi:
- Setiap berkas yang dipertontonkan dihitung hash SHA-256-nya.
- Tidak ada angka "progress" yang dikarang di UI: semuanya dibaca dari
  status.json, dan status.json ikut ditampilkan isinya.
- Dashboard tidak boleh mengklaim sesuatu yang tidak bisa dicek di repo.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import html
import time
import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent          # .../ai-belajar-dari-ai
DASH = ROOT / "dashboard"
PLAN = ROOT / "PLAN.md"
AUDIT = ROOT / "docs" / "self-audit-v0.2.md"
POLICY = ROOT / "ops" / "policy.v1.json"
REPORT = ROOT / "REPORT-P0.md"
P0_METRICS = ROOT / "runs" / "p0" / "metrics.jsonl"
PREREG = ROOT / "PREREGISTRATION.md"
PREREG_LOCK = ROOT / "ops" / "prereg.lock.json"
STATUS = DASH / "status.json"
TEMPLATE = DASH / "template.html"
STYLE = DASH / "assets" / "style.css"
APP = DASH / "assets" / "app.js"
OUT = DASH / "index.html"

TEXT_SUFFIXES = {".md", ".json", ".py", ".css", ".js", ".html", ".yaml", ".yml", ".txt", ".jsonl", ".toml"}
MAX_INLINE_BYTES = 400_000


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# util
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as fh:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 16), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def human_bytes(n: int) -> str:
    for unit in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB"):
        if n < 1024 or unit == "GB":
            return f"{n:.0f} {unit}" if unit == "B" else f"{n:.1f} {unit}"
        n /= 1024
    return f"{n:.1f} GB"


def slug(text: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9\s-]", "", text.lower())
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", "-", s.strip())
    return s or "bagian"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# markdown -> html (subset yang dipakai PLAN.md)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def inline(text: str) -> str:
    """Escape lalu terapkan markdown inline."""
    codes: list[str] = []

    def stash(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        codes.append(m.group(1))
        return f"\x00{len(codes) - 1}\x00"

    text = re.sub(r"`([^`]+)`", stash, text)
    text = html.escape(text, quote=False)
    text = re.sub(r"\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)", r'<a href="\2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">\1</a>', text)
    # bold dulu (boleh memuat *italic* di dalamnya), baru italic sisanya
    text = re.sub(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*", r"<strong>\1</strong>", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(?<!\*)\*([^*\n]+)\*(?!\*)", r"<em>\1</em>", text)
    text = re.sub(r"~~([^~]+)~~", r"<del>\1</del>", text)

    def unstash(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        return f"<code>{html.escape(codes[int(m.group(1))], quote=False)}</code>"

    return re.sub(r"\x00(\d+)\x00", unstash, text)


def md_to_html(md: str, demote: int = 1) -> tuple[str, list[dict]]:
    lines = md.splitlines()
    out: list[str] = []
    toc: list[dict] = []
    used: dict[str, int] = {}
    i = 0
    n = len(lines)

    def heading(level: int, text: str) -> None:
        base = slug(re.sub(r"`|\*\*|\*", "", text))
        count = used.get(base, 0)
        used[base] = count + 1
        anchor = base if count == 0 else f"{base}-{count}"
        lvl = min(6, level + demote)
        out.append(f'<h{lvl} id="{anchor}">{inline(text)}<a class="anchor" href="#{anchor}">#</a></h{lvl}>')
        if lvl <= 3:
            toc.append({"level": lvl, "anchor": anchor, "text": re.sub(r"`", "", text)})

    while i < n:
        line = lines[i]

        if line.startswith("```"):
            lang = line.strip("`").strip().lower()
            i += 1
            buf = []
            while i < n and not lines[i].startswith("```"):
                buf.append(lines[i])
                i += 1
            i += 1
            body = html.escape(chr(10).join(buf), quote=False)
            if lang == "mermaid":
                # mermaid butuh library eksternal; kita tidak memuat apa pun dari internet.
                out.append(
                    '<div class="callout" style="margin:14px 0"><strong>Diagram alur.</strong> '
                    'Versi visualnya ada di <a href="#denah">bagian 03 — Yang akan dibangun</a>. '
                    'Sumber aslinya (mermaid) tetap disimpan di bawah agar tidak ada yang disembunyikan.'
                    f'<details class="raw" style="margin:10px 0 0"><summary>sumber diagram (mermaid)</summary>'
                    f"<pre><code>{body}</code></pre></details></div>"
                )
            else:
                out.append(f"<pre><code>{body}</code></pre>")
            continue

        if not line.strip():
            i += 1
            continue

        m = re.match(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$", line)
        if m:
            heading(len(m.group(1)), m.group(2).strip())
            i += 1
            continue

        if re.match(r"^\s*(---|\*\*\*|___)\s*$", line):
            out.append("<hr>")
            i += 1
            continue

        # tabel
        if line.lstrip().startswith("|") and i + 1 < n and re.match(r"^\s*\|[\s:|-]+\|\s*$", lines[i + 1]):
            header = [c.strip() for c in line.strip().strip("|").split("|")]
            i += 2
            rows = []
            while i < n and lines[i].lstrip().startswith("|"):
                rows.append([c.strip() for c in lines[i].strip().strip("|").split("|")])
                i += 1
            out.append("<table><thead><tr>")
            out.extend(f"<th>{inline(c)}</th>" for c in header)
            out.append("</tr></thead><tbody>")
            for r in rows:
                out.append("<tr>" + "".join(f"<td>{inline(c)}</td>" for c in r) + "</tr>")
            out.append("</tbody></table>")
            continue

        # blockquote
        if line.startswith(">"):
            buf = []
            while i < n and lines[i].startswith(">"):
                buf.append(lines[i].lstrip(">").strip())
                i += 1
            out.append(f"<blockquote>{inline(' '.join(buf))}</blockquote>")
            continue

        # list
        if re.match(r"^\s*([-*+]|\d+\.)\s+", line):
            stack: list[str] = []
            while i < n and re.match(r"^\s*([-*+]|\d+\.)\s+", lines[i]):
                raw = lines[i]
                indent = len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip())
                ordered = bool(re.match(r"^\s*\d+\.\s+", raw))
                tag = "ol" if ordered else "ul"
                depth = indent // 2
                while len(stack) > depth:
                    out.append(f"</li></{stack.pop()}>")
                if len(stack) == depth:
                    if stack:
                        out.append("</li>")
                    out.append(f"<{tag}>")
                    stack.append(tag)
                body = re.sub(r"^\s*([-*+]|\d+\.)\s+", "", raw)
                out.append(f"<li>{inline(body)}")
                i += 1
            while stack:
                out.append(f"</li></{stack.pop()}>")
            continue

        buf = []
        while i < n and lines[i].strip() and not re.match(
            r"^(#{1,6}\s|\s*\||```|>|\s*([-*+]|\d+\.)\s+)", lines[i]
        ) and not re.match(r"^\s*(---|\*\*\*|___)\s*$", lines[i]):
            buf.append(lines[i].strip())
            i += 1
        out.append(f"<p>{inline(' '.join(buf))}</p>")

    return "\n".join(out), toc


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# inventaris berkas
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {"data", "runs", "checkpoints", "__pycache__", ".git", ".venv"}


def inventory() -> list[dict]:
    items = []
    for path in sorted(ROOT.rglob("*")):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT)
        if path.name == "index.html" or path.name.startswith(".DS"):
            continue
        if EXCLUDE_DIRS & set(rel.parts):
            continue
        size = path.stat().st_size
        binary = path.suffix not in TEXT_SUFFIXES
        items.append(
            {
                "path": rel.as_posix(),
                "abs": str(path),
                "bytes": size,
                "size": human_bytes(size),
                "sha256": sha256(path),
                "binary": binary,
                "content": None if binary or size > MAX_INLINE_BYTES else path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"),
            }
        )
    return items


def esc(s: str) -> str:
    return html.escape(s, quote=True)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# render
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def main() -> None:
    plan_text = PLAN.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    plan_html, plan_toc = md_to_html(plan_text, demote=1)
    status = json.loads(STATUS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    files = inventory()

    p = status["progress"]
    meta = status["meta"]

    # --- kartu status jujur
    cards = [
        ("Rencana", f"{p['plan_pct']}%", "dokumen rencana (v" + meta["plan_version"] + ")", "ok"),
        ("Implementasi", f"{p['implementation_pct']}%", "belum ada kode eksperimen", "zero"),
        ("Model terlatih", str(p["models_trained"]), "dari target 32 chain / 13 arm", "zero"),
        ("Token digenerate", f"{p['tokens_generated']:,}".replace(",", "."), "dari target ±350 juta token sintetis", "zero"),
        ("Checkpoint", str(p["checkpoints"]), "belum ada artefak model", "zero"),
        ("Baris kode eksperimen", str(p["lines_of_experiment_code"]), "0 baris ditulis", "zero"),
    ]
    cards_html = "".join(
        f'<div class="stat {cls}"><div class="stat-label">{esc(l)}</div>'
        f'<div class="stat-value">{esc(v)}</div><div class="stat-sub">{esc(s)}</div></div>'
        for l, v, s, cls in cards
    )

    # --- fase
    badge = {
        "belum-mulai": ("belum mulai", "badge-idle"),
        "berjalan": ("berjalan", "badge-run"),
        "selesai": ("selesai", "badge-done"),
        "diblokir": ("menunggu keputusanmu", "badge-wait"),
    }
    phase_rows = []
    for ph in status["phases"]:
        label, cls = badge.get(ph["status"], (ph["status"], "badge-idle"))
        phase_rows.append(
            "<tr>"
            f'<td class="mono">{esc(ph["id"])}</td>'
            f'<td><strong>{esc(ph["name"])}</strong><div class="muted small">{esc(ph.get("desc", ""))}</div></td>'
            f'<td><span class="badge {cls}">{esc(label)}</span></td>'
            f'<td class="small">{esc(ph.get("output", "—"))}</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono">{esc(ph.get("evidence", "—"))}</td>'
            "</tr>"
        )
    phases_html = (
        "<table><thead><tr><th>Fase</th><th>Nama</th><th>Status</th>"
        "<th>Luaran</th><th>Bukti di repo</th></tr></thead><tbody>"
        + "".join(phase_rows)
        + "</tbody></table>"
    )

    # --- berkas terencana
    planned_rows = []
    for f in status["planned_files"]:
        label, cls = badge.get(f["status"], (f["status"], "badge-idle"))
        planned_rows.append(
            "<tr>"
            f'<td class="mono small">{esc(f["path"])}</td>'
            f'<td class="small">{esc(f["purpose"])}</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono">{esc(f.get("phase", "—"))}</td>'
            f'<td><span class="badge {cls}">{esc(label)}</span></td>'
            "</tr>"
        )
    planned_html = "".join(planned_rows)

    # --- keputusan
    dec_html = []
    default_note = ' <em class="muted">(default-ku)</em>'
    for d in status["decisions"]:
        opts = "".join(
            '<li><span class="mono small">{}</span> {}{}</li>'.format(
                esc(o["id"]), esc(o["label"]), default_note if o.get("default") else ""
            )
            for o in d["options"]
        )
        dec_html.append(
            f'<article class="card decision"><div class="decision-head">'
            f'<span class="badge badge-wait">{esc(d["status"])}</span>'
            f'<h4>{esc(d["question"])}</h4></div>'
            f'<ul class="tight">{opts}</ul>'
            f'<p class="small muted">Dampak: {esc(d["impact"])}</p></article>'
        )
    decisions_html = "".join(dec_html)

    # --- changelog
    log_html = []
    for c in sorted(status["changelog"], key=lambda x: x["date"], reverse=True):
        log_html.append(
            '<li class="log-item">'
            f'<time class="mono small">{esc(c["date"])}</time>'
            f'<div><strong>{esc(c["title"])}</strong>'
            f'<p class="small">{esc(c["detail"])}</p>'
            f'<p class="small mono muted">bukti: {esc(c["evidence"])}</p></div></li>'
        )
    changelog_html = "<ul class='log'>" + "".join(log_html) + "</ul>"

    # --- klaim vs verifikasi
    claims_html = "".join(
        f'<tr><td class="small">{esc(c["claim"])}</td>'
        f'<td class="small mono cmd" data-cmd="{esc(c["verify"])}">{esc(c["verify"])}</td></tr>'
        for c in status["claims"]
    )

    # --- bias
    bias_html = "".join(
        f'<li><strong>{esc(b["title"])}</strong><p class="small">{esc(b["detail"])}</p></li>'
        for b in status["bias_notes"]
    )

    # --- literatur
    lit_html = "".join(
        f'<li><a href="{esc(l["url"])}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">{esc(l["title"])}</a> '
        f'<span class="muted small">— {esc(l["authors"])}, {esc(str(l["year"]))}</span>'
        f'<p class="small muted">{esc(l["why"])}</p></li>'
        for l in status["literature"]
    )

    # --- status pra-registrasi (dihitung ulang saat build, bukan diketik)
    pr = {"version": "-", "hash": "-", "state": "belum dikunci", "class": "zero"}
    if PREREG_LOCK.exists() and PREREG.exists():
        import json as _json
        rec = _json.loads(PREREG_LOCK.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        live = sha256(PREREG)
        ok = live == rec.get("sha256")
        pr = {
            "version": rec.get("version", "?"),
            "hash": rec.get("sha256", "?")[:32] + "…",
            "state": "cocok dengan kunci" if ok else "TIDAK COCOK dengan kunci",
            "class": "ok" if ok else "zero",
        }

    # --- ringkasan artefak hasil run (tidak di-inline agar halaman tetap ringan)
    art_rows = []
    for sub in ("runs", "data", "checkpoints"):
        base = ROOT / sub
        if not base.exists():
            continue
        fs = [f for f in base.rglob("*") if f.is_file()]
        if not fs:
            continue
        tot = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in fs)
        newest = max(f.stat().st_mtime for f in fs)
        art_rows.append(
            "<tr>"
            f'<td class="mono small">{esc(sub)}/</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono">{len(fs)}</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono">{human_bytes(tot)}</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono">{time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M", time.localtime(newest))}</td>'
            "</tr>"
        )
    artifacts_html = ""
    if art_rows:
        artifacts_html = (
            '<p class="small muted">Artefak hasil run sengaja <strong>tidak</strong> di-inline ke halaman ini '
            "(ukurannya puluhan MB). Yang ditampilkan hanya jumlah berkas, total byte, dan waktu modifikasi "
            "terakhir; isinya bisa kamu buka langsung dari disk.</p>"
            "<table><thead><tr><th>Direktori</th><th>Berkas</th><th>Total</th><th>Terakhir diubah</th></tr></thead>"
            "<tbody>" + "".join(art_rows) + "</tbody></table>"
        )

    # --- inventaris berkas + penampil isi
    file_rows = []
    viewers = []
    for f in files:
        view = "teks" if f["content"] is not None else "biner/terlalu besar"
        file_rows.append(
            "<tr>"
            f'<td class="mono small">{esc(f["path"])}</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono">{esc(f["size"])}</td>'
            f'<td class="small mono hash">{esc(f["sha256"][:16])}…</td>'
            f'<td class="small">{view}</td>'
            "</tr>"
        )
        if f["content"] is not None:
            viewers.append(
                f'<details class="viewer"><summary class="mono">{esc(f["path"])} '
                f'<span class="muted small">· {esc(f["size"])} · sha256 {esc(f["sha256"][:12])}…</span></summary>'
                f'<pre><code>{html.escape(f["content"], quote=False)}</code></pre></details>'
            )
    files_html = (
        artifacts_html
        + '<p class="small muted">Berkas sumber di bawah ini di-inline seluruhnya (kecuali biner dan berkas '
        + "di atas " + human_bytes(MAX_INLINE_BYTES) + ").</p>"
        + "<table><thead><tr><th>Berkas</th><th>Ukuran</th><th>SHA-256</th><th>Isi</th></tr></thead><tbody>"
        + "".join(file_rows)
        + "</tbody></table>"
    )
    viewers_html = "".join(viewers)

    # --- daftar isi plan
    toc_html = "".join(
        f'<a class="toc-l{item["level"]}" href="#{item["anchor"]}">{esc(item["text"])}</a>'
        for item in plan_toc
        if item["level"] <= 3
    )

    # --- angka ringkas P0 (dibaca dari metrik mentah, bukan dari status.json)
    p0 = {"models": "-", "mu": "-", "sigma": "-", "thr": "-", "delta": "-", "tokens": "-"}
    if P0_METRICS.exists():
        import json as _json
        import statistics as _st
        rows = [_json.loads(l) for l in P0_METRICS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
        base = [r for r in rows if r["arm"] == "baseline"]
        chains = [r for r in rows if r["arm"] != "baseline"]
        mu = _st.mean(r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in base)
        sg = _st.stdev([r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in base])
        worst = max(chains, key=lambda r: r["gate"]["delta_bpb"])
        p0 = {
            "models": str(len(rows)),
            "mu": f"{mu:.4f}",
            "sigma": f"{sg:.4f}",
            "thr": f"{worst['gate']['threshold_bpb']:.4f}",
            "delta": f"{worst['gate']['delta_bpb']:+.4f}",
            "tokens": f"{sum(r.get('tokens_generated', 0) for r in chains):,}".replace(",", "."),
        }

    # --- laporan P0
    report_html, report_toc = ("", [])
    if REPORT.exists():
        report_html, report_toc = md_to_html(REPORT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), demote=1)

    # --- audit mandiri + kebijakan kegagalan
    audit_html, audit_toc = ("", [])
    policy_table, policy_meta = "", ""
    if AUDIT.exists():
        audit_html, audit_toc = md_to_html(AUDIT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), demote=1)
    if POLICY.exists():
        pol = json.loads(POLICY.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        sev_badge = {"critical": "badge-wait", "high": "badge-wait", "medium": "badge-run", "low": "badge-idle"}
        auth_badge = {"human": "badge-wait", "autonomous": "badge-done"}
        rows = []
        for f in pol["failures"]:
            rows.append(
                "<tr>"
                f'<td class="mono small">{esc(f["id"])}</td>'
                f'<td class="small">{esc(f["name"])}</td>'
                f'<td><span class="badge {sev_badge.get(f["severity"], "badge-idle")}">{esc(f["severity"])}</span></td>'
                f'<td><span class="badge {auth_badge.get(f["autonomy"], "badge-idle")}">{esc(f["autonomy"])}</span></td>'
                f'<td class="small mono">{esc(" -> ".join(f["route"]))}</td>'
                f'<td class="small mono">{esc(str(f["recovery_minutes"]))} mnt</td>'
                "</tr>"
            )
        policy_table = (
            "<table><thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Kegagalan</th><th>Keparahan</th>"
            "<th>Kewenangan</th><th>Rute</th><th>MTTR</th></tr></thead><tbody>"
            + "".join(rows) + "</tbody></table>"
        )
        n_auto = len(pol["autonomy"]["autonomous"])
        n_human = len(pol["autonomy"]["human"])
        policy_meta = (
            f'{len(pol["failures"])} mode kegagalan · {n_auto} aksi otonom · {n_human} aksi manusia · '
            f'kill switch {len(pol["kill_switch"]["triggers"])} pemicu (otoritas: {pol["kill_switch"]["authority"]})'
        )

    build_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")
    plan_hash = sha256(PLAN)
    status_hash = sha256(STATUS)

    replacements = {
        "{{TITLE}}": esc(meta["project"]),
        "{{IMPL_PCT}}": str(p["implementation_pct"]),
        "{{LOC}}": str(p["lines_of_experiment_code"]),
        "{{MODELS}}": str(p["models_trained"]),
        "{{BUILD_TIME}}": build_time,
        "{{PLAN_VERSION}}": esc(meta["plan_version"]),
        "{{PLAN_STATUS}}": esc(meta["plan_status"]),
        "{{BRANCH}}": esc(meta["branch"]),
        "{{ROOT_ABS}}": esc(str(ROOT)),
        "{{PLAN_HASH}}": plan_hash,
        "{{STATUS_HASH}}": status_hash,
        "{{PLAN_LINES}}": str(len(plan_text.splitlines())),
        "{{PLAN_TOKENS}}": f"{len(plan_text.split()):,}".replace(",", "."),
        "{{CARDS}}": cards_html,
        "{{PHASES}}": phases_html,
        "{{PLANNED_FILES}}": planned_html,
        "{{DECISIONS}}": decisions_html,
        "{{CHANGELOG}}": changelog_html,
        "{{CLAIMS}}": claims_html,
        "{{BIAS}}": bias_html,
        "{{LITERATURE}}": lit_html,
        "{{FILES}}": files_html,
        "{{VIEWERS}}": viewers_html,
        "{{PLAN_HTML}}": plan_html,
        "{{PLAN_TOC}}": toc_html,
        "{{FILE_COUNT}}": str(len(files)),
        "{{TOTAL_BYTES}}": human_bytes(sum(f["bytes"] for f in files)),
        "{{HEADLINE}}": esc(meta["headline"]),
        "{{READ_ME_FIRST}}": esc(meta["read_me_first"]),
        "{{PREREG_VERSION}}": pr["version"],
        "{{PREREG_HASH}}": pr["hash"],
        "{{PREREG_STATE}}": pr["state"],
        "{{PREREG_CLASS}}": pr["class"],
        "{{P0_MODELS}}": p0["models"],
        "{{P0_MU}}": p0["mu"],
        "{{P0_SIGMA}}": p0["sigma"],
        "{{P0_THR}}": p0["thr"],
        "{{P0_DELTA}}": p0["delta"],
        "{{P0_TOKENS}}": p0["tokens"],
        "{{REPORT_HTML}}": report_html,
        "{{REPORT_TOC}}": "".join(
            f'<a class="toc-l{item["level"]}" href="#{item["anchor"]}">{esc(item["text"])}</a>'
            for item in report_toc if item["level"] <= 3
        ),
        "{{AUDIT_HTML}}": audit_html,
        "{{AUDIT_TOC}}": "".join(
            f'<a class="toc-l{item["level"]}" href="#{item["anchor"]}">{esc(item["text"])}</a>'
            for item in audit_toc if item["level"] <= 3
        ),
        "{{POLICY_TABLE}}": policy_table,
        "{{POLICY_META}}": esc(policy_meta),
        "{{POLICY_HASH}}": sha256(POLICY) if POLICY.exists() else "-",
    }

    page = TEMPLATE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    css = STYLE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    js = APP.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    page = page.replace("{{CSS}}", css).replace("{{JS}}", js)
    for key, value in replacements.items():
        if key == "{{VIEWERS}}":
            continue  # disisipkan terakhir: isinya berkas mentah, jangan ikut di-substitusi
        page = page.replace(key, value)
    page = page.replace("{{VIEWERS}}", viewers_html)

    OUT.write_text(page, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"OK  {OUT}")
    print(f"    berkas diinventarisasi : {len(files)}")
    print(f"    sha256 PLAN.md         : {plan_hash[:16]}…")
    print(f"    sha256 status.json     : {status_hash[:16]}…")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

      <h2><span class="idx">01</span>Status jujur</h2>

        Angka di bawah dibaca dari <code>dashboard/status.json</code>, bukan diketik ke dalam HTML.

        Saat ini yang ada hanyalah rencana dan halaman ini — tidak ada kode eksperimen, tidak ada model, tidak ada data yang dihasilkan.
      </p>
