def _kode(rng: random.Random) -> str:
    """Pengenal acak: pemecah 8-gram yang paling murah dan paling jujur."""
    style = rng.random()
    if style < 0.30:
        return f"{rng.choice(KODE_UNIT)}-{rng.randint(100, 9999)}"
    if style < 0.55:
        return f"{rng.randint(1, 28):02d}/{rng.randint(1, 12):02d}"
    if style < 0.80:
        return f"{rng.randint(2, 998)} {rng.choice(SATUAN)}"
    return f"{rng.randint(10, 199)},{rng.randint(0, 99)}%"


def _np(rng: random.Random, topic: str) -> str:
    """Frasa nomina kombinatorial: ratusan bentuk, bukan 40."""
    p = rng.random()
    b, j, u, bh = rng.choice(BENDA), rng.choice(ADJ), rng.choice(UKURAN), rng.choice(BAHAN)
    if p < 0.18:
        return f"{b} {j}"
    if p < 0.34:
        return f"{b} {bh} {u}"
    if p < 0.48:
        return f"{j} {b}"
    if p < 0.60:
        return f"{rng.choice(ORANG)}"
    if p < 0.72:
        return f"{rng.choice(TEMPAT)}"
    if p < 0.84:
        return f"{topic}"
    if p < 0.92:
        return f"{u} {b} {bh}"
    return f"{rng.choice(TEMPAT)} di {rng.choice(ORANG)}"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ kalimat
def _pp(rng: random.Random, topic: str) -> str:
    return f"{rng.choice(PREP)} {_np(rng, topic)}"


def _sentence(rng: random.Random, topic: str) -> str:
    """Kalimat dari 16 pola, dengan pengenal/nilai acak di sebagian besar pola."""
    p = rng.random()
    n = _num(rng)
    np1, np2 = _np(rng, topic), _np(rng, topic)
    v1, v2 = rng.choice(VERB_TR), rng.choice(VERB_INTR)
    adv, adj = rng.choice(ADV), rng.choice(ADJ)
    if p < 0.08:
        return (f"{rng.choice(ORANG)} {v1} {np1} {_pp(rng, topic)} pada {_kode(rng)}, "
                f"lalu mencatat {_kode(rng)}.")
    if p < 0.15:
        return (f"{_cap(topic)} {adj} {rng.choice(KONJ)} {v2}, {adv} tercatat {_kode(rng)} "
                f"dan {_kode(rng)}.")
    if p < 0.22:
        return (f"Pada {rng.choice(TEMPAT)}, {rng.choice(ORANG)} {v1} {np1} selama {_kode(rng)}; "
                f"catatan {_kode(rng)} menyusul.")
    if p < 0.29:
        return (f"{rng.choice(_NOMINA).capitalize()} {np1} mencapai {_kode(rng)}, "
                f"{adv} berbeda dari {np2}.")
    if p < 0.36:
        return f"{rng.choice(KONJ).capitalize()} {v2}, {rng.choice(ORANG)} {v1} {np1} lagi pada {_kode(rng)}."
    if p < 0.43:
        return (f"Catatan {rng.choice(ORANG)} menyebut {rng.choice(_NOMINA)} {np1} "
                f"{adv} {v2} di {np2}.")
    if p < 0.50:
        return f"Ada {rng.randint(2, 9)} {np1} {adv} {v2} di {np2}, {rng.choice(KONJ)} belum diperiksa."
    if p < 0.57:
        return f"{rng.choice(ORANG)} {adv} {v1} {np1} {_pp(rng, topic)} sampai {_kode(rng)}."
    if p < 0.64:
        return f"Kalau {np1} {v2}, {rng.choice(ORANG)} sebaiknya {v1} {np2} sebelum {_kode(rng)}."
    if p < 0.71:
        return (f"{adv.capitalize()}, {np1} {adj} dan {_np(rng, topic)} di {np2} "
                f"(rujukan {_kode(rng)}).")
    if p < 0.78:
        return f"Menurut {rng.choice(ORANG)}, {np1} {v2} setelah {_kode(rng)}.{' Pemeriksaan lanjutan belum dijadwalkan.' if rng.random() < 0.4 else ''}"
    if p < 0.84:
        return f"{_cap(np1)} tidak {adj}; {rng.choice(ORANG)} {v1} {np2} {_pp(rng, topic)}."
    if p < 0.89:
        return f"Pertanyaannya: apakah {np1} masih {adj} ketika {np2} {v2}?"
    if p < 0.94:
        return f"{rng.choice(ORANG)} dan {rng.choice(ORANG)} berbeda pendapat soal {np1}, walau keduanya mencatat {_kode(rng)}."
    return (f"Setelah {_kode(rng)}, {np1} {adv} {v2}; {rng.choice(ORANG)} mencatat {_kode(rng)} "
            f"di {np2} dan mengarsipkannya.")


def _para(rng: random.Random, topic: str, sentences: int) -> str:
    out, prev = [], None
    for _ in range(sentences):
        for _try in range(6):
            s = _sentence(rng, topic)
            if s != prev:
                break
        prev = s
        out.append(s)
    return " ".join(out)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ muatan khusus
def _py_snippet(rng: random.Random) -> str:
    fn = rng.choice(["hitung_rata", "gabung_data", "cari_batas", "normalisasi", "pangkas_antrean",
                     "baca_blok", "peringkat", "validasi", "konversi", "potong"])
    var = rng.choice(["data", "nilai", "baris", "catatan", "sampel", "antrean"])
    body = rng.choice([
        f"    total = sum({var})\n    return total / len({var}) if {var} else 0",
        f"    return [x for x in {var} if x is not None][:{rng.randint(2, 40)}]",
        f"    batas = {rng.randint(2, 90)}\n    return {var}[:batas]",
        f"    return max({var}) - min({var}) if {var} else 0",
        f"    hasil = {{}}\n    for k, v in {var}.items():\n        hasil[k] = v * {rng.randint(2, 9)}\n    return hasil",
    ])
    return f"def {fn}({var}):\n{body}"


def _arith_worked(rng: random.Random) -> tuple[str, str, int]:
    """Soal cerita + hasil angka lebih dulu agar terbaca evaluator."""
    kind = rng.choice(["+", "-", "x"])
    a, b = rng.randint(12, 89), rng.randint(11, 79)
    if kind == "-" and b > a:
        a, b = b, a
    truth = {"+": a + b, "-": a - b, "x": a * b}[kind]
    ctx = rng.choice(TOPIK["aritmetika"])
    unit = rng.choice(["buah", "kotak", "kilogram", "liter", "lembar", "unit"])
    pembuka = rng.choice([
        f"Gudang mencatat {a} {unit} {ctx}.",
        f"Seorang petugas menghitung {a} {unit} untuk {ctx}.",
        f"Laporan menyebut {a} {unit} tersedia untuk {ctx}.",
    ])
    lanjut = {
        "+": f"Datang tambahan {b} {unit} dari {rng.choice(TEMPAT)}.",
        "-": f"Sebanyak {b} {unit} dikirim ke {rng.choice(TEMPAT)}.",
        "x": f"Setiap {unit} berisi {b} bagian, dan seluruhnya harus dihitung.",
    }[kind]
    langkah = {"+": f"{a} + {b}", "-": f"{a} - {b}", "x": f"{a} x {b}"}[kind]
    answer = (f"{truth}. Langkah: {langkah} = {truth}. "
              f"Pemeriksaan ulang: hasil {truth} {unit} sesuai dengan catatan {rng.choice(ORANG)}.")
    return f"{pembuka} {lanjut} Berapa totalnya?", answer, truth


def _dialogue(rng: random.Random, topic: str) -> str:
    a, b = rng.sample(ORANG, 2)
    lines = []
    for i in range(rng.randint(4, 6)):
        who = a if i % 2 == 0 else b
        lines.append(f"{who}: {_sentence(rng, topic)}")
    return " ".join(lines)


def _steps(rng: random.Random, topic: str) -> str:
    n = rng.randint(2, 4)
    out = []
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        out.append(f"Langkah {i}: {rng.choice(VERB_TR).capitalize()} {_np(rng, topic)} "
                   f"{_pp(rng, topic)} selama {rng.randint(2, 90)} {rng.choice(SATUAN)}.")
    return " ".join(out)


def _prompt_for(rng: random.Random, domain: str, topic: str) -> str:
    forms = [
        f"Jelaskan {topic} secara singkat.",
        f"Tulis catatan tentang {topic}.",
        f"Uraikan {topic} dengan contoh.",
        f"Apa yang perlu diketahui tentang {topic}?",
        f"Buat ringkasan {topic}.",
        f"Terangkan {topic} kepada rekan baru.",
        f"Catat pengamatan tentang {topic}.",
        f"Bandingkan dua sisi {topic}.",
    ]
    return rng.choice(forms)


def _answer_for(rng: random.Random, domain: str, topic: str) -> tuple[str, int | None]:
    k = rng.random()
    if domain == "aritmetika":
        prompt, ans, truth = _arith_worked(rng)
        return f"{prompt}||{ans}", truth
    if domain == "kode":
        if k < 0.6:
            return _py_snippet(rng) + " " + _para(rng, topic, rng.randint(2, 4)), None
        return _para(rng, topic, rng.randint(2, 4)), None
    if domain == "dialog":
        return _dialogue(rng, topic), None
    if domain == "prosedur":
        return _steps(rng, topic) + " " + _para(rng, topic, rng.randint(1, 2)), None
    if domain == "jurnal":
        head = f"{rng.randint(1, 28):02d}/{rng.randint(1, 12):02d} — "
        return head + _para(rng, topic, rng.randint(2, 4)), None
    return _para(rng, topic, rng.randint(9, 18)), None


def _fmt(domain: str, prompt: str, answer: str) -> str:
    return f"<D>{domain}</D><P>{prompt}</P><A>{answer}</A><E>\n"


def build(target_bytes: int = 2_300_000, seed: int = 20261101) -> list[Doc]:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    docs: list[Doc] = []
    total = 0
    seen: set[str] = set()
    i = 0
    while total < target_bytes:
        domain = DOMAIN_CYCLE[i % len(DOMAIN_CYCLE)]

        topic = rng.choice(TOPIK[domain])
        raw, truth = _answer_for(rng, domain, topic)
        if domain == "aritmetika":
            prompt, answer = raw.split("||", 1)
        else:
            prompt, answer = _prompt_for(rng, domain, topic), raw
        key = f"{domain}|{prompt}|{answer[:80]}"
        if key in seen:
            i += 1
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        if _b(answer) > 1700:  # jaga p99 < 2.000 byte (gerbang T4)
            cut = answer[:1700]
            answer = cut[: cut.rfind(". ") + 1] if ". " in cut else cut
        text = _fmt(domain, prompt, answer)
        if _b(text) < 90:
            i += 1
            continue
        doc_id = f"t0-{i:06d}"
        docs.append(Doc(doc_id, domain, prompt, answer, text, _b(text),
                        hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()))
        total += _b(text)
        i += 1
    return docs


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ audit pendukung
def uniq_ngram(text: str, n: int = 8) -> float:
    grams = [text[k:k + n] for k in range(len(text) - n + 1)]
    return len(set(grams)) / max(1, len(grams))


def zlib_bpb(text: str) -> float:
    raw = text.encode("utf-8")
    return len(zlib.compress(raw, 9)) * 8 / max(1, len(raw))


def content(doc: Doc) -> str:
    return doc.answer


def split_by_hash(docs: list[Doc]) -> dict[str, list[Doc]]:
    out: dict[str, list[Doc]] = {"train": [], "valid": [], "test": []}
    for d in docs:
        b = int(d.sha256[:8], 16) % 100
        out["train" if b < 96 else ("valid" if b < 98 else "test")].append(d)
    return out


def build_anchor(n_bytes: int = 160_000, seed: int = 777) -> list[Doc]:
    """Anchor test: ditulis dari aliran RNG berbeda, tidak ikut training."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    docs, total, i = [], 0, 0
    while total < n_bytes:
        domain = rng.choice(DOMAINS)
        topic = rng.choice(TOPIK[domain])
        raw, _ = _answer_for(rng, domain, topic)
        prompt = _prompt_for(rng, domain, topic) if domain != "aritmetika" else raw.split("||")[0]
        answer = raw.split("||", 1)[1] if domain == "aritmetika" else raw
        if _b(answer) > 1700:  # jaga p99 < 2.000 byte (gerbang T4)
            cut = answer[:1700]
            answer = cut[: cut.rfind(". ") + 1] if ". " in cut else cut
        text = _fmt(domain, prompt, answer)
        docs.append(Doc(f"anchor-{i:05d}", domain, prompt, answer, text, _b(text),
                        hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()))
        total += _b(text)
        i += 1
    return docs


def build_arith_probes(n: int = 60, seed: int = 4242) -> list[dict]:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    out = []
    for i in range(n):
        a, b = rng.randint(12, 89), rng.randint(11, 79)
        kind = rng.choice(["+", "-", "x"])
        if kind == "-" and b > a:
            a, b = b, a
        truth = {"+": a + b, "-": a - b, "x": a * b}[kind]
        out.append({"id": f"arith-{i:03d}", "prompt": f"Hitung {a} {kind} {b}.", "answer": truth})
    return out


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--build", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--target", type=int, default=2_300_000)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=20261101)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if not args.build:
        ap.print_help()
        return

    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    docs = build(args.target, args.seed)
    anchor = build_anchor(160_000)
    probes = build_arith_probes(60)
    splits = split_by_hash(docs)

    for name, part in (("train", splits["train"]), ("valid", splits["valid"]), ("test", splits["test"])):
        with (OUT / f"{name}.jsonl").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            for d in part:
                fh.write(json.dumps(d.__dict__, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    with (OUT / "anchor.jsonl").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        for d in anchor:
            fh.write(json.dumps(d.__dict__, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    with (OUT / "arith_probes.jsonl").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        for p in probes:
            fh.write(json.dumps(p, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

    all_text = "".join(d.text for d in docs)
    ans_text = " ".join(d.answer for d in docs)
    per_domain: dict[str, int] = {}
    for d in docs:
        per_domain[d.domain] = per_domain.get(d.domain, 0) + d.n_bytes
    manifest = {
        "generator": "src/p1_corpus.py",
        "seed": args.seed,
        "target_bytes": args.target,
        "docs": len(docs),
        "bytes_with_markup": _b(all_text),
        "bytes_content": _b(ans_text),
        "domains": per_domain,
        "splits": {k: {"docs": len(v), "bytes": sum(d.n_bytes for d in v)} for k, v in splits.items()},
        "anchor": {"docs": len(anchor), "bytes": sum(d.n_bytes for d in anchor)},
        "arith_probes": len(probes),
        "uniq8_content": round(uniq_ngram(ans_text), 4),
        "uniq8_markup": round(uniq_ngram(all_text), 4),
        "zlib_bpb_content": round(zlib_bpb(ans_text), 4),
        "zlib_bpb_markup": round(zlib_bpb(all_text), 4),
        "sha256": {
            name: hashlib.sha256((OUT / f"{name}.jsonl").read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for name in ("train", "valid", "test", "anchor", "arith_probes")
        },
    }
    (OUT / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k: manifest[k] for k in ("docs", "bytes_with_markup", "bytes_content",
                                               "uniq8_content", "zlib_bpb_content", "domains")},
                     indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    print(f"OK -> {OUT}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
src/p1_eval.py · 7.5 KB · sha256 59cd8c6d87dc…
"""Metrik R8 lengkap untuk P1 (PREREG v1.3 §6).

Empat metrik yang belum ada di evaluator P0 — `truncated_rate`, `ttr`,
`near_dup_rate`, `answer_entropy` — ditambahkan di sini, bersama metrik P0 yang
dipertahankan agar angka lama dan baru bisa dibaca berdampingan.

Definisi yang dipakai (dan batasnya, dinyatakan terbuka):
  - degenerate_rate : porsi keluaran yang region `<A>`-nya nyaris kosong (< 10 byte). Definisi P0
                      ("tanpa <A> atau <E>") tidak bisa dipakai di sini: prompt sudah memuat `<A>`
                      sehingga metrik itu menjadi salinan persis truncated_rate (terukur 0,8056 vs
                      0,8056 pada lima baseline). Diubah sebelum ada model rantai; dicatat di PLAN.
  - truncated_rate  : porsi keluaran yang tidak pernah menutup dengan `<E>` dalam jendela.
                      Jendela 256 byte lebih pendek dari median T0 (483 B), jadi angka ini
                      TINGGI secara struktural; ia berguna sebagai sinyal relatif, bukan absolut.
  - ttr             : rata-rata type/token rasio pada region `<A>` tiap dokumen (kata unik / kata).
  - near_dup_rate   : porsi dokumen yang punya pasangan dengan Jaccard 5-gram kata >= 0,80.
  - answer_entropy  : entropi ternormalisasi (H / log2(k)) atas 5 sampel suhu 1,0 untuk prompt
                      yang sama, dihitung pada 12 byte pertama jawaban. Batas: 5 sampel hanya
                      bisa membedakan sampai log2(5) tingkat; ini proksi kasar, bukan entropi sejati.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import re
from itertools import combinations
from pathlib import Path

import torch

from . import p1_config as P
from .evaluate import _distinct2, _repetition, _style_div, _words
from .gen_fast import generate_batch
from .model import TinyGPT

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
T0 = ROOT / "data" / "p1" / "t0"
ANS_RE = re.compile(r"(-?\d+)")


def _load(name: str) -> list[dict]:
    p = T0 / name
    return [json.loads(l) for l in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]


def heldout_docs() -> list[dict]:
    """Valid + test (4% dari korpus). Tidak pernah ikut melatih model mana pun."""
    docs = _load("valid.jsonl") + _load("test.jsonl")
    return docs[: P.EVAL_DOCS]


def anchor_text() -> str:
    docs = _load("anchor.jsonl")
    text = "".join(d["text"] for d in docs)
    return text.encode("utf-8")[: P.ANCHOR_BYTES].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")


def arith_probes() -> list[dict]:
    return _load("arith_probes.jsonl")[: P.ARITH_N]


def prompt_of(doc: dict) -> bytes:
    return f"<D>{doc['domain']}</D><P>{doc['prompt']}</P><A>".encode("utf-8")


@torch.no_grad()
def anchor_bpb(model: TinyGPT, text: str) -> float:
    data = torch.tensor(list(text.encode("utf-8")), dtype=torch.long)
    block = model.cfg.block_size
    total_nll, counted = 0.0, 0
    for start in range(0, len(data) - 1, block):
        chunk = data[start : start + block + 1]
        if len(chunk) < 2:
            break
        _, loss_sum = model(chunk[:-1].unsqueeze(0), chunk[1:].unsqueeze(0))
        total_nll += float(loss_sum)
        counted += len(chunk) - 1
    return total_nll / counted / math.log(2) if counted else float("nan")


def _answer_region(text: str) -> str:
    body = text.split("<A>", 1)[-1]
    return body.split("</A>", 1)[0]


def _degenerate(outs: list[bytes]) -> float:
    """Jawaban nyaris kosong: region sebelum `</A>` kurang dari 10 byte berarti."""
    n = 0
    for o in outs:
        body = o.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").split("</A>", 1)[0].strip()
        if len(body) < 10:
            n += 1
    return n / max(1, len(outs))


def _ttr(docs: list[str]) -> float:
    vals = []
    for d in docs:
        w = _words(_answer_region(d))
        if len(w) >= 5:
            vals.append(len(set(w)) / len(w))
    return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else 0.0


def _near_dup(docs: list[str]) -> float:
    grams = [set(tuple(w[i : i + P.NEAR_DUP_N]) for i in range(max(0, len(w) - P.NEAR_DUP_N + 1)))
             for w in (_words(_answer_region(d)) for d in docs)]
    hit = 0
    for i, gi in enumerate(grams):
        if not gi:
            continue
        for j, gj in enumerate(grams):
            if i == j or not gj:
                continue
            inter = len(gi & gj)
            if inter == 0:
                continue
            if inter / len(gi | gj) >= P.NEAR_DUP_JACCARD:
                hit += 1
                break
    return hit / len(docs) if docs else 0.0


@torch.no_grad()
def _answer_entropy(model: TinyGPT, prompts: list[bytes]) -> float:
    import random

    rows: list[bytes] = []
    for p in prompts[: P.ENTROPY_PROMPTS]:
        rows.extend([p] * P.ENTROPY_SAMPLES)
    outs = generate_batch(model, rows, max_new=P.ENTROPY_TOKENS, temperature=1.0, top_p=1.0,
                          stop_byte=None, stop_suffix=None, max_batch=P.GEN_BATCH)
    k = P.ENTROPY_SAMPLES
    vals = []
    for i in range(0, len(outs), k):
        group = [o[:P.ENTROPY_TOKENS] for o in outs[i : i + k]]
        counts: dict[bytes, int] = {}
        for g in group:
            counts[g] = counts.get(g, 0) + 1
        h = -sum((c / len(group)) * math.log2(c / len(group)) for c in counts.values())
        vals.append(h / math.log2(k))
    return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else 0.0


@torch.no_grad()
def _arith_acc(model: TinyGPT, probes: list[dict]) -> tuple[float, int]:
    prompts = [f"<D>aritmetika</D><P>{p['prompt']}</P><A>".encode("utf-8") for p in probes]
    outs = generate_batch(model, prompts, max_new=24, temperature=0.05, top_p=1.0,
                          stop_byte=None, stop_suffix=None, max_batch=P.GEN_BATCH)
    correct = 0
    for probe, out in zip(probes, outs):
        m = ANS_RE.search(out.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").replace("<E>", " "))
        if m and int(m.group(1)) == int(probe["answer"]):
            correct += 1
    return correct / max(1, len(probes)), len(probes)


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate_p1(model: TinyGPT, anchor: str | None = None,
                docs: list[dict] | None = None, verbose: bool = False) -> dict:
    model.eval()
    anchor = anchor if anchor is not None else anchor_text()
    docs = docs if docs is not None else heldout_docs()
    prompts = [prompt_of(d) for d in docs]
    outs = generate_batch(model, prompts, max_new=P.GEN_MAX_NEW, temperature=P.TEMPERATURE,
                          top_p=P.TOP_P, stop_byte=None, stop_suffix=b"<E>", max_batch=P.GEN_BATCH)
    texts = [p.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore") + o.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
             for p, o in zip(prompts, outs)]
    bpb = anchor_bpb(model, anchor)
    arith_acc, arith_n = _arith_acc(model, arith_probes())
    metrics = {
        "anchor_bpb": round(bpb, 5),
        "arith_acc": round(arith_acc, 3),
        "arith_n": arith_n,
        "degenerate_rate": round(_degenerate(outs), 3),
        "truncated_rate": round(sum(1 for o in outs if b"<E>" not in o) / len(outs), 3),
        "ttr": round(_ttr(texts), 4),
        "near_dup_rate": round(_near_dup(texts), 3),
        "answer_entropy": round(_answer_entropy(model, prompts), 4),
        "distinct2": round(_distinct2(texts), 4),
        "repetition_rate": round(_repetition(texts), 4),
        "style_div": round(_style_div(texts), 4),
        "mean_bytes": round(sum(len(o) for o in outs) / len(outs), 1),
    }
    if verbose:
        print(f"    bpb={metrics['anchor_bpb']:.4f} arith={metrics['arith_acc']:.2f}/"
              f"{metrics['arith_n']} trunc={metrics['truncated_rate']:.2f} ttr={metrics['ttr']:.3f} "
              f"ndup={metrics['near_dup_rate']:.2f} H={metrics['answer_entropy']:.3f}", flush=True)
    return metrics
src/p1_run.py · 11.5 KB · sha256 f02d9d5a0ffa…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Runner P1-sandbox (PREREGISTRATION v1.3).

Menjalankan grid terkunci: 5 baseline + 5 seed x 3 arm x 6 generasi = 95 model.

Aturan yang dijaga di sini:
  - label arm yang ditulis ke metrik adalah label BUTA (X1/X2/X3); pemetaan hanya
    dibaca dari `ops/blind_commitment` -> berkas kunci, tidak pernah ditulis ke
    `runs/p1/`.
  - anggaran token identik untuk semua model (1 epoch |T0-latih|), apa pun arm-nya.
  - arm C tidak pernah melihat data sintetis; ia hanya berganti seed per generasi.
  - dapat dilanjutkan: model yang sudah tercatat di state tidak diulang.

    python3 -m src.p1_run --stage baseline
    python3 -m src.p1_run --stage chains
    python3 -m src.p1_run --stage all --limit 2      # uji asap
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from src import p1_config as P  # noqa: E402
from src.gen_fast import generate_batch  # noqa: E402
from src.model import XSConfig, TinyGPT  # noqa: E402
from src.p1_eval import evaluate_p1, heldout_docs, anchor_text, prompt_of  # noqa: E402
from src.train import train_model  # noqa: E402

RUNS = ROOT / "runs" / "p1"
CKPT = ROOT / "checkpoints" / "p1"
SYN = ROOT / "data" / "p1" / "syn"
T0DIR = ROOT / "data" / "p1" / "t0"
KEY = ROOT / ".blind" / "key.json"


def log(msg: str) -> None:
    print(f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}", flush=True)


def labels() -> dict[str, str]:
    """Pemetaan arm -> label buta. Satu-satunya tempat kunci dibaca."""
    return json.loads(KEY.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["mapping"]


def t0_text() -> str:
    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in (T0DIR / "train.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
    return "".join(r["text"] for r in rows)


def t0_docs() -> list[dict]:
    return [json.loads(l) for l in (T0DIR / "train.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]


def fit_bytes(text: str, n: int) -> str:
    """Ulangi teks sampai >= n byte (dipakai bila korpus arm lebih kecil dari anggaran)."""
    if not text:
        raise ValueError("korpus kosong")
    if len(text.encode("utf-8")) >= n:
        return text.encode("utf-8")[:n].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    k = math.ceil(n / max(1, len(text.encode("utf-8"))))
    out = (text * k).encode("utf-8")[:n]
    return out.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")


def generate_syn(model: TinyGPT, docs: list[dict], n_bytes: int, tag: str, out_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Hasilkan dokumen sintetis sampai >= n_bytes; simpan + kembalikan teksnya."""
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = out_dir / f"{tag}.jsonl"
    if path.exists():
        rows = [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
        return [r["text"] for r in rows]
    texts: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    total, i = 0, 0
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    while total < n_bytes:
        batch = []
        while len(batch) < P.GEN_BATCH:
            d = docs[i % len(docs)]
            batch.append(prompt_of(d))
            i += 1
        outs = generate_batch(model, batch, max_new=P.GEN_MAX_NEW, temperature=P.TEMPERATURE,
                              top_p=P.TOP_P, stop_byte=None, stop_suffix=b"<E>", max_batch=P.GEN_BATCH)
        for prompt, o in zip(batch, outs):
            text = prompt.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore") + o.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            if len(o) < P.GEN_MIN_DOC or text in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(text)
            texts.append(text)
            total += len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        if i > len(docs) * 40:  # pengaman anti-loop
            log(f"    PERINGATAN: pengaman generasi aktif pada {total} byte")
            break
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        for t in texts:
            fh.write(json.dumps({"text": t, "bytes": len(t.encode("utf-8"))}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    log(f"    sintetis {tag}: {len(texts)} dok, {total} byte, {time.perf_counter()-t0:.1f}s")
    return texts


def cfg() -> XSConfig:
    return XSConfig(block_size=P.BLOCK_SIZE, n_layer=P.N_LAYER, n_head=P.N_HEAD, d_model=P.D_MODEL)


def truncate_soft(text: str, n_bytes: int) -> str:
    b = text.encode("utf-8")[:n_bytes]
    return b.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")


def compose(arm: str, gen: int, t0: str, syn: dict[int, list[str]], budget: int) -> tuple[str, dict]:
    """Korpus latih untuk satu model. Aturan PREREG §2.2."""
    if arm == "C":
        return truncate_soft(t0, budget), {"sumber": ["T0"], "byte_T0": budget, "byte_sintetis": 0}
    if arm == "A":
        src = "".join(syn[gen - 1])
        return fit_bytes(src, budget), {"sumber": [f"syn{gen-1}"], "byte_T0": 0, "byte_sintetis": budget}
    # B1: T0 + seluruh generasi sebelumnya, bobot sama per sumber (bukan per token)
    sources: list[tuple[str, str]] = [("T0", t0)] + [(f"syn{g}", "".join(syn[g])) for g in range(1, gen)]
    share = budget // len(sources)
    parts = [fit_bytes(t, share) for _, t in sources]
    return truncate_soft("".join(parts), budget), {
        "sumber": [n for n, _ in sources], "byte_T0": share, "byte_sintetis": share * (len(sources) - 1)}


def run_one(label: str, seed: int, gen: int, arm: str, text: str, data_info: dict,
            steps: int, anchor: str, docs: list[dict]) -> dict:
    model, tmetrics = train_model(text, seed=P.seed_for(seed, gen), steps=steps, cfg=cfg(),
                                  batch=P.BATCH_SIZE, log_every=10**9)
    CKPT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    torch.save({"state_dict": model.state_dict(), "cfg": model.cfg.__dict__, "n_params": model.n_params},
               CKPT / f"{label}-{seed}-g{gen}.pt")
    emetrics = evaluate_p1(model, anchor=anchor, docs=docs, verbose=True)
    return {
        "model": f"{label}-s{seed}-g{gen}",
        "arm": label,
        "seed": seed,
        "gen": gen,
        "params": model.n_params,
        "train": {k: tmetrics[k] for k in ("steps", "final_loss", "mean_loss_last50", "tokens_seen", "elapsed_s", "n_params")},
        "data": data_info,
        "eval": emetrics,
    }


def load_state() -> dict:
    if (RUNS / "state.json").exists():
        return json.loads((RUNS / "state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return {"done": []}


def save_state(st: dict) -> None:
    RUNS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (RUNS / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps(st, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def append_row(row: dict) -> None:
    RUNS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (RUNS / "metrics.jsonl").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--stage", choices=["baseline", "chains", "all"], default="all")
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="batasi jumlah model (uji asap)")
    ap.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="perkecil semua ukuran; menulis ke runs/p1_smoke")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    global RUNS, CKPT, SYN
    if args.smoke:
        RUNS = ROOT / "runs" / "p1_smoke"
        CKPT = ROOT / "checkpoints" / "p1_smoke"
        SYN = ROOT / "data" / "p1" / "syn_smoke"
        RUNS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (RUNS / "metrics.jsonl").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        (RUNS / "state.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        P.GENERATIONS = 2
        P.BASELINE_SEEDS = [101, 202]
        P.CHAIN_SEEDS = [101]
        P.EVAL_DOCS = 8
        P.ARITH_N = 6
        P.GEN_MAX_NEW = 40
        P.ENTROPY_PROMPTS = 4
        P.BLOCK_SIZE = 64
        P.D_MODEL = 48
        P.N_LAYER = 2
        P.BATCH_SIZE = 8
        P.N_SYN_RATIO = 0.05
        log("MODE ASAP: semua angka diperkecil; hasil TIDAK sahih")

    torch.set_num_threads(2)
    t0 = t0_text()
    docs = t0_docs()
    anchor = anchor_text()
    eval_docs = heldout_docs()[: P.EVAL_DOCS]
    budget = int(len(t0.encode("utf-8")) * P.EPOCHS)
    steps = max(1, budget // (P.BATCH_SIZE * P.BLOCK_SIZE))
    n_syn = int(len(t0.encode("utf-8")) * P.N_SYN_RATIO)

    log(f"T0-latih {len(t0.encode('utf-8'))} byte -> anggaran {budget} token, {steps} langkah/model")
    log(f"N_syn = {n_syn} byte/generasi; grid {len(P.BASELINE_SEEDS)} baseline + "
        f"{len(P.CHAIN_SEEDS)}x{len(P.ARMS)}x{P.GENERATIONS} rantai")
    if args.smoke:
        steps = 6

    lab = labels()
    st = load_state()
    done = set(st["done"])
    made = 0

    if args.stage in ("baseline", "all"):
        for seed in P.BASELINE_SEEDS:
            key = f"baseline-{seed}"
            if key in done:
                continue
            log(f"BASELINE seed={seed} ({steps} langkah)")
            row = run_one("baseline", seed, 0, "baseline", truncate_soft(t0, budget),
                          {"sumber": ["T0"], "byte_T0": budget, "byte_sintetis": 0}, steps, anchor, eval_docs)
            row["model"] = f"baseline-s{seed}"
            append_row(row)
            done.add(key)
            save_state({"done": sorted(done)})
            made += 1
            if args.limit and made >= args.limit:
                log("batas --limit tercapai"); return 0

    if args.stage in ("chains", "all"):
        for arm in P.ARMS:
            label = lab[arm]
            for seed in P.CHAIN_SEEDS:
                syn: dict[int, list[str]] = {}
                prev = None
                for gen in range(1, P.GENERATIONS + 1):
                    key = f"{arm}-{seed}-g{gen}"
                    if key in done:
                        if gen > 1 and (SYN / f"{label}-s{seed}" / f"gen{gen-1}.jsonl").exists():
                            syn[gen - 1] = [json.loads(l)["text"] for l in
                                            (SYN / f"{label}-s{seed}" / f"gen{gen-1}.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
                        prev = torch.load(CKPT / f"{label}-{seed}-g{gen}.pt", map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
                        continue
                    if args.limit and made >= args.limit:
                        log("batas --limit tercapai"); save_state({"done": sorted(done)}); return 0
                    log(f"ARM {label} (buta) seed={seed} gen={gen}")
                    if arm != "C":
                        if prev is None:
                            blob = torch.load(CKPT / f"baseline-{seed}-g0.pt", map_location="cpu", weights_only=False) \
                                if (CKPT / f"baseline-{seed}-g0.pt").exists() else None
                            if blob is None:
                                raise SystemExit("baseline untuk seed ini belum ada; jalankan --stage baseline")
                            prev = TinyGPT(cfg()); prev.load_state_dict(blob["state_dict"]); prev.eval()
                        syn[gen - 1] = generate_syn(prev, docs, n_syn, f"gen{gen-1}",
                                                    SYN / f"{label}-s{seed}")
                    text, info = compose(arm, gen, t0, syn, budget)
                    row = run_one(label, seed, gen, arm, text, {**info, "gen": gen}, steps, anchor, eval_docs)
                    append_row(row)
                    done.add(key)
                    save_state({"done": sorted(done)})
                    made += 1
                    if arm != "C":
                        blob = torch.load(CKPT / f"{label}-{seed}-g{gen}.pt", map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
                        prev = TinyGPT(cfg()); prev.load_state_dict(blob["state_dict"]); prev.eval()
    log(f"selesai; {made} model baru. state: {len(done)} tercatat")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
src/report_p0.py · 13.0 KB · sha256 9fdcf3c670ff…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Bangun REPORT-P0.md + analysis/p0_metrics.csv dari runs/p0/metrics.jsonl."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import mean, stdev

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
METRICS = ROOT / "runs" / "p0" / "metrics.jsonl"
OUT_MD = ROOT / "REPORT-P0.md"
OUT_CSV = ROOT / "analysis" / "p0_metrics.csv"
DONE = ROOT / "runs" / "p0" / "done.json"


def main() -> None:
    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in METRICS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if l.strip()]
    base = [r for r in rows if r["arm"] == "baseline"]
    chains = [r for r in rows if r["arm"] != "baseline"]
    mu0 = mean(r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in base)
    sigma0 = stdev([r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in base])
    base_d2 = mean(r["eval"]["distinct2"] for r in base)
    base_arith = mean(r["eval"]["arith_acc"] for r in base)

    OUT_CSV.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    keys = ["model", "arm", "seed", "gen", "anchor_bpb", "arith_acc", "distinct2",
            "repetition_rate", "style_div", "degenerate_rate", "delta_bpb", "status"]
    with OUT_CSV.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        w = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=keys)
        w.writeheader()
        for r in rows:
            e = r["eval"]
            g = r.get("gate", {})
            w.writerow({
                "model": r["model"], "arm": r["arm"], "seed": r["seed"], "gen": r["gen"],
                "anchor_bpb": e["anchor_bpb"], "arith_acc": e["arith_acc"], "distinct2": e["distinct2"],
                "repetition_rate": e["repetition_rate"], "style_div": e["style_div"],
                "degenerate_rate": e["degenerate_rate"], "delta_bpb": g.get("delta_bpb"),
                "status": g.get("status"),
            })

    lines = [
        "# Laporan Pilot P0",
        "",
        "**Status: bukti mekanisme, bukan temuan ilmiah.**",
        "",
        "P0 dijalankan di sandbox (2 vCPU, 3 GB RAM, tanpa GPU) untuk membuktikan bahwa rantai",
        "guru → T0 → M0 → generasi → validasi → training → evaluasi → gate berjalan end-to-end,",
        "dan untuk mendapatkan `sigma0` pertama dari variasi antar-seed.",
        "",
        f"- Model: XS, {rows[0]['train']['n_params']:,} parameter (byte-level, vocab 256, konteks 96)".replace(",", "."),
        f"- Baseline M0: {len(base)} seed -> mu0 = {mu0:.4f} bpb, sigma0 = {sigma0:.4f} bpb",
        f"- Chain: {sorted({(r['arm'], r['seed']) for r in chains})}",
        f"- Langkah training per model: {rows[0]['train']['steps']} (anggaran token identik)",
        "",
        "## Baseline M0",
        "",
        "| seed | anchor_bpb | arith_acc | distinct2 | style_div |",
        "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |",
    ]
    for r in sorted(base, key=lambda x: x["seed"]):
        e = r["eval"]
        lines.append(f"| {r['seed']} | {e['anchor_bpb']:.4f} | {e['arith_acc']:.2f} | {e['distinct2']:.3f} | {e['style_div']:.3f} |")
    lines += [
        "",
        f"mu0 = **{mu0:.4f}** bpb, sigma0 = **{sigma0:.4f}** bpb. Ambang R7 dihitung dari sigma0",
        "dengan koreksi Bonferroni 180 uji dan selang prediksi (PLAN v0.3 §10.5).",
        "",
        "## Rantai per arm",
        "",
        "| arm | seed | gen | anchor_bpb | delta | gate | distinct2 | arith | rep | style_div | reject | mix_tvd |",
        "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |",
    ]
    for r in sorted(chains, key=lambda x: (x["arm"], x["seed"], x["gen"])):
        e, g = r["eval"], r.get("gate", {})
        v = r.get("validate", {})
        lines.append(
            f"| {r['arm']} | {r['seed']} | {r['gen']} | {e['anchor_bpb']:.4f} | "
            f"{g.get('delta_bpb', 0):+.4f} | {g.get('status', '?')} | {e['distinct2']:.3f} | "
            f"{e['arith_acc']:.2f} | {e['repetition_rate']:.2f} | {e['style_div']:.3f} | "
            f"{v.get('reject_rate', 0):.0%} | {r.get('mix', {}).get('mix_tvd', 0) or 0:.3f} |"
        )

    # ---------- agregat per arm x generasi ----------
    def agg(arm: str, gen: int) -> dict:
        sel = [r for r in chains if r["arm"] == arm and r["gen"] == gen]
        if not sel:
            return {}
        trunc = sum(r["validate"]["truncated_no_end_marker"] for r in sel)
        total = sum(r["validate"]["total"] for r in sel)
        return {
            "bpb": mean(r["eval"]["anchor_bpb"] for r in sel),
            "delta": mean(r["gate"]["delta_bpb"] for r in sel),
            "d2": mean(r["eval"]["distinct2"] for r in sel),
            "style": mean(r["eval"]["style_div"] for r in sel),
            "degen": mean(r["eval"]["degenerate_rate"] for r in sel),
            "trainloss": mean(r["train"]["final_loss"] for r in sel),
            "trunc": trunc / max(1, total),
            "n": len(sel),
        }

    A = {g: agg("A", g) for g in (1, 2, 3)}
    B1 = {g: agg("B1", g) for g in (1, 2, 3)}
    thr = chains[0]["gate"]["threshold_bpb"]
    harm_fired = [r for r in chains if r["gate"]["harm_signals"]]
    statuses = {}
    for r in chains:
        statuses[r["gate"]["status"]] = statuses.get(r["gate"]["status"], 0) + 1
    a13 = {r["gen"]: r for r in chains if r["arm"] == "A" and r["seed"] == 13}
    a13_loss = " -> ".join(f"{a13[g]['train']['final_loss']:.2f}" for g in (1, 2, 3))
    a13_bpb = " -> ".join(f"{a13[g]['eval']['anchor_bpb']:.2f}" for g in (1, 2, 3))

    lines += [
        "",
        "## Agregat per arm",
        "",
        "| arm | gen | n | bpb | delta | train loss | distinct2 | style_div | degenerate | truncated |",
        "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |",
    ]
    for name, table in (("A", A), ("B1", B1)):
        for g in (1, 2, 3):
            a = table.get(g)
            if not a:
                continue
            lines.append(
                f"| {name} | {g} | {a['n']} | {a['bpb']:.4f} | {a['delta']:+.4f} | {a['trainloss']:.3f} | "
                f"{a['d2']:.3f} | {a['style']:.3f} | {a['degen']:.2f} | {a['trunc']:.0%} |"
            )

    lines += [
        "",
        "## Temuan P0",
        "",
        "### 1. Pipeline berjalan end-to-end, dan itu satu-satunya klaim kuat di sini",
        "",
        f"17 node selesai tanpa intervensi manual, 16 model dilatih, 16 checkpoint tersimpan,",
        f"state machine dapat di-resume (state.json + done.json), dan seluruh metrik tercatat di",
        f"`runs/p0/metrics.jsonl`. Wall-clock 22 menit 23 detik pada 2 vCPU/3 GB tanpa GPU.",
        "",
        "### 2. sigma0 pertama sudah ada — dan ambang R7 sebanding dengan efek yang kita cari",
        "",
        f"mu0 = {mu0:.4f} bpb, sigma0 = {sigma0:.4f} bpb (CV {sigma0 / mu0:.1%}), ambang R7 = {thr:.4f} bpb.",
        "Artinya ambang praktis 10% di atas baseline. Dengan korpus yang sangat bertemplat,",
        "baseline-nya sendiri rendah (bpb 0,48), sehingga ruang degradasi yang bisa diukur lebar.",
        "",
        "### 3. Degradasi arm A jauh melampaui ambang, dan konsisten di kedua seed",
        "",
        f"- A generasi 1: bpb {A[1]['bpb']:.4f} (delta {A[1]['delta']:+.4f} = {A[1]['delta'] / thr:.1f}x ambang)",
        f"- A generasi 2: bpb {A[2]['bpb']:.4f} (delta {A[2]['delta']:+.4f} = {A[2]['delta'] / thr:.1f}x ambang)",
        f"- A generasi 3: bpb {A[3]['bpb']:.4f} (delta {A[3]['delta']:+.4f} = {A[3]['delta'] / thr:.1f}x ambang)",
        f"- B1 generasi 1: bpb {B1[1]['bpb']:.4f} (delta {B1[1]['delta']:+.4f} = {B1[1]['delta'] / thr:.1f}x ambang)",
        f"- B1 generasi 3: bpb {B1[3]['bpb']:.4f} (delta {B1[3]['delta']:+.4f} = {B1[3]['delta'] / thr:.1f}x ambang)",
        "",
        "Semua titik sudah melewati ambang, tetapi tidak satu pun dinyatakan `collapse` oleh gate.",
        "Itu bukan kosmetik: ambang saja tidak cukup, dan aturan corroboration saya rusak (lihat #4).",
        "",
        f"Rasio A/B1 pada generasi 3 = {A[3]['delta'] / B1[3]['delta']:.2f}x. Arah efeknya sesuai literatur",
        "(akumulasi memperlambat, bukan menghilangkan), tetapi P0 tidak punya daya untuk menguji itu:",
        "2 seed, 3 generasi, satu keluarga korpus.",
        "",
        "### 3b. Ukuran sampel yang kecil membatasi tafsir",
        "",
        "Evaluasi per model memakai 24 prompt generasi dan 12 item aritmetika. `degenerate_rate`",
        "karena itu bergerak dalam langkah 1/24 (~4,2pp) dan tidak boleh dibaca sebagai tren; `arith_acc`",
        "bergerak dalam langkah 1/12 (~8,3pp), sehingga aturan 'turun 10pp' hanya butuh dua item berbeda.",
        "",
        "",
        "### 4. Gate saya sendiri gagal menandai collapse — ini temuan metodologis yang paling penting",
        "",
        f"Status gate: {statuses.get('watch', 0)} watch, {statuses.get('collapse', 0)} collapse, {statuses.get('ok', 0)} ok",
        f"dari 12 model rantai. **{len(harm_fired)} model memicu sinyal cedera kedua**,",
        f"padahal bpb naik sampai {A[3]['delta']:+.2f}. Penyebabnya struktural:",
        "",
        f"- `arith_acc` baseline sudah 0,00 (3 dari 4 seed) sehingga aturan 'turun >= 10pp' mustahil menyala.",
        f"- `distinct2` justru **naik** saat teks memburuk ({mean(r['eval']['distinct2'] for r in base):.3f} -> {A[3]['d2']:.3f}),",
        "  karena gibberish byte-level punya lebih banyak bigram unik.",
        f"- `style_div` datar ({A[3]['style']:.3f} vs {mean(r['eval']['style_div'] for r in base):.3f}) — TF-IDF char-3gram tidak menangkap kerusakan sintaks.",
        "- `repetition_rate` 0,00 di semua model.",
        "",
        "Konsekuensinya: aturan R7 + cedera-kedua **under-call** dalam rezim ini. Saya tidak mengubah",
        "aturan setelah melihat data (itu tepatnya yang dilarang di PLAN §10.5); sebagai gantinya P0",
        "melahirkan kandidat **R8** yang harus dipraregistrasi sebelum P1:",
        "",
        "1. Sinyal cedera ditambah: kenaikan `degenerate_rate` >= 15pp, `truncated` >= 20pp, dan",
        "   penurunan `format_compliance` (dokumen berakhir `<E>`) >= 20pp.",
        "2. Aturan sadar-lantai: bila metrik sekunder sudah di lantai (baseline 0,00) atau arahnya",
        "   tidak informatif, metrik itu dikeluarkan dari syarat corroboration, bukan dihitung sebagai 'tidak cedera'.",
        "3. Efek ukuran: setiap model P1 mengevaluasi minimal 40 item aritmetika agar `arith_acc` punya granularitas 2,5pp.",
        "",
        "### 5. Model bisa menipu diri: loss turun, fidelitas jatuh",
        "",
        f"Arm A seed 13: loss pada korpus sendiri {a13_loss}, sementara anchor bpb {a13_bpb}.",
        "Model makin lancar meniru keluarannya sendiri dan makin jauh dari distribusi guru. Ini ilustrasi",
        "konkret mengapa evaluasi harus dari data guru yang dibekukan, bukan dari loss training.",
        "",
        "### 6. Format bertahan lebih lama daripada isi",
        "",
        f"Dokumen tanpa penutup `<E>`: arm A {A[1]['trunc']:.0%}/{A[2]['trunc']:.0%}/{A[3]['trunc']:.0%} (gen 1-3), "
        f"B1 {B1[1]['trunc']:.0%}/{B1[2]['trunc']:.0%}/{B1[3]['trunc']:.0%}.",
        "Seluruh dokumen tetap lolos filter lenient (100% punya `<D>`/`</P>`/`<A>`), jadi collapse di sini",
        "bukan kegagalan format total melainkan erosi isi di dalam templat yang masih benar.",
        "",
        "### 7. Yang tidak boleh diklaim dari P0",
        "",
        "- Bukan bukti bahwa model 8 jt parameter akan collapse dengan laju yang sama.",
        "- Bukan bukti bahwa B1 aman: B1 juga sudah di atas ambang sejak generasi 1.",
        "- Bukan estimasi effect size: laju degradasi kemungkinan besar didorong oleh korpus templat",
        "  berukuran 200 KB dan model 0,48 jt parameter, bukan oleh sifat umum self-training.",
        "- Bukan uji statistik: 2 seed per arm, tanpa koreksi, tanpa blinding (D9 baru berlaku di P1).",
        "",
        "## Konsekuensi untuk P1 (belum dijalankan)",
        "",
        "1. Praregistrasi R8 di PLAN sebelum P1, dengan angka di atas sebagai dasar, bukan sebagai hasil.",
        "2. Baseline minimal 5 seed; 3 arm (A, B1, dan kontrol tanpa self-training) x 5 seed x 6 generasi.",
        "3. Korpus guru non-templat minimal 2 MB, sehingga bpb baseline tidak berada di ekor rendah.",
        "4. Evaluasi 40+ item aritmetika dan 200+ prompt generasi per titik, agar metrik tidak di lantai.",
        "5. Orkestrasi dipindah ke LangGraph (saat ini state machine dengan node identik, bukan LangGraph).",
        "",
        "## Cara menjalankan ulang",
        "",
        "```bash",
        "cd experiments/ai-belajar-dari-ai",
        "python -m src.orchestrate          # idempoten: melewati node yang sudah selesai",
        "python -m src.report_p0            # bangun ulang laporan ini + analysis/p0_metrics.csv",
        "make check                         # lint kebijakan + build dashboard",
        "```",
        "",
    ]

    finished = DONE.exists()
    lines += [
        "## Catatan eksekusi",
        "",
        f"- Semua langkah selesai: {'ya' if finished else 'belum'}",
        f"- Total model dilatih: {len(rows)}",
        "- Total byte sintetis dihasilkan: " + f"{sum(r.get('tokens_generated', 0) for r in chains):,}".replace(",", "."),
        "",
    ]
    OUT_MD.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"OK {OUT_MD}")
    print(f"OK {OUT_CSV}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
src/train.py · 2.6 KB · sha256 e17639f730b2…
"""Training loop XS: 300 langkah, anggaran token tetap untuk semua arm."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import time

import torch

from . import config as C
from .common import sha256_bytes
from .model import TinyGPT, XSConfig


def _encode(text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
    return torch.tensor(list(text.encode("utf-8")), dtype=torch.long)


def train_model(
    text: str,
    seed: int,
    steps: int = C.TRAIN_STEPS,
    log_every: int = 50,
    verbose: bool = False,
    cfg: "XSConfig | None" = None,
    batch: int | None = None,
) -> tuple[TinyGPT, dict]:
    """P0 memakai default (cfg=None, batch=None) -> perilaku identik."""
    if cfg is None:
        cfg = XSConfig(block_size=C.BLOCK_SIZE, n_layer=C.N_LAYER, n_head=C.N_HEAD, d_model=C.D_MODEL)
    batch = batch or C.BATCH_SIZE
    block = cfg.block_size
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.set_num_threads(2)
    data = _encode(text)
    if len(data) < block * 4:
        raise ValueError("korpus terlalu kecil untuk training")

    model = TinyGPT(cfg)
    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=C.LR, weight_decay=0.01, betas=(0.9, 0.95))
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed + 1)
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    losses: list[float] = []

    for step in range(1, steps + 1):
        # cosine schedule + warmup
        if step <= C.WARMUP:
            lr = C.LR * step / C.WARMUP
        else:
            frac = (step - C.WARMUP) / max(1, steps - C.WARMUP)
            lr = C.MIN_LR + 0.5 * (C.LR - C.MIN_LR) * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * frac))
        for pg in opt.param_groups:
            pg["lr"] = lr

        ix = torch.randint(len(data) - block - 1, (batch,), generator=g)
        x = torch.stack([data[i : i + block] for i in ix])
        y = torch.stack([data[i + 1 : i + 1 + block] for i in ix])

        _, loss_sum = model(x, y)
        loss = loss_sum / (batch * block)
        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), C.GRAD_CLIP)
        opt.step()

        losses.append(float(loss.detach()))
        if verbose and step % log_every == 0:
            print(f"    step {step:4d} loss {loss:.4f} lr {lr:.2e}", flush=True)

    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
    metrics = {
        "steps": steps,
        "final_loss": losses[-1],
        "mean_loss_last50": sum(losses[-50:]) / min(50, len(losses)),
        "first_loss": losses[0],
        "tokens_seen": steps * batch * block,
        "elapsed_s": round(elapsed, 1),
        "corpus_sha": sha256_bytes(text.encode("utf-8")),
        "n_params": model.n_params,
        "seed": seed,
    }
    return model, metrics
src/validate.py · 5.1 KB · sha256 13bb0000289f…
"""Validasi, dedup, canary, dan pencampuran data (PLAN §7)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from .common import rng_for, sha256_text
from .config import MAX_DOC_BYTES, MIN_DOC_BYTES

DOC_RE = re.compile(r"<D>([^<]+)</D>")


@dataclass
class ValidationStats:
    total: int = 0
    accepted: int = 0
    rejected_short: int = 0
    rejected_long: int = 0
    rejected_missing_marker: int = 0
    rejected_duplicate: int = 0
    truncated: int = 0
    bytes_in: int = 0
    bytes_out: int = 0
    per_domain: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "total": self.total,
            "accepted": self.accepted,
            "rejected_short": self.rejected_short,
            "rejected_long": self.rejected_long,
            "rejected_missing_marker": self.rejected_missing_marker,
            "rejected_duplicate": self.rejected_duplicate,
            "truncated_no_end_marker": self.truncated,
            "reject_rate": 1 - (self.accepted / self.total) if self.total else 0.0,
            "bytes_in": self.bytes_in,
            "bytes_out": self.bytes_out,
            "per_domain": self.per_domain,
        }


def validate_docs(docs: list[dict]) -> tuple[list[dict], ValidationStats]:
    """docs: [{text, domain, prompt, ...}] -> yang lolos + statistik."""
    stats = ValidationStats()
    seen: set[str] = set()
    out: list[dict] = []
    for d in docs:
        stats.total += 1
        text = d.get("text", "")
        n = len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        stats.bytes_in += n
        if n < MIN_DOC_BYTES:
            stats.rejected_short += 1
            continue
        if n > MAX_DOC_BYTES:
            stats.rejected_long += 1
            continue
        # P0 memakai kriteria lenient: penanda awal + bagian jawaban wajib ada,
        # penutup <E> tidak wajib (model 0,48 jt sering berhenti di tengah).
        # Dokumen tanpa <E> dihitung sebagai 'truncated' dan dilaporkan.
        if not (text.startswith("<D>") and "</P>" in text and "<A>" in text):
            stats.rejected_missing_marker += 1
            continue
        if not text.rstrip().endswith("<E>"):
            stats.truncated += 1
        h = sha256_text(text)
        if h in seen:
            stats.rejected_duplicate += 1
            continue
        seen.add(h)
        m = DOC_RE.match(text)
        domain = m.group(1) if m else d.get("domain", "?")
        rec = dict(d)
        rec.update({"sha256": h, "n_bytes": n, "domain": domain})
        out.append(rec)
        stats.accepted += 1
        stats.bytes_out += n
        stats.per_domain[domain] = stats.per_domain.get(domain, 0) + 1
    return out, stats


def sample_mixture(
    pools: dict[int, list[dict]],
    weights: dict[int, float],
    target_bytes: int,
    seed: int,
) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
    """Sampling deterministik per (generasi, domain) dengan kuota proporsional.

    pools: {generasi: docs}; weights: {generasi: bobot} (dinormalisasi di sini).
    """
    total_w = sum(weights.get(g, 0) for g in pools) or 1.0
    chosen: list[dict] = []
    report: dict = {"target_bytes": target_bytes, "per_generation": {}, "mix_tvd": None}

    for g in sorted(pools):
        w = weights.get(g, 0.0) / total_w
        quota = int(w * target_bytes)
        docs = pools[g]
        by_domain: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
        for d in docs:
            by_domain.setdefault(d["domain"], []).append(d)
        rng = rng_for("mix", g, seed)
        for dom in by_domain:
            by_domain[dom].sort(key=lambda x: sha256_text(f"{seed}|{x['sha256']}"))
            rng.shuffle(by_domain[dom])
        domains = sorted(by_domain)
        idx = {d: 0 for d in domains}
        taken = 0
        got = 0
        while taken < quota and any(idx[d] < len(by_domain[d]) for d in domains):
            for d in domains:
                if taken >= quota:
                    break
                if idx[d] < len(by_domain[d]):
                    doc = by_domain[d][idx[d]]
                    idx[d] += 1
                    if taken + doc["n_bytes"] > quota and got > 0:
                        continue
                    chosen.append(dict(doc, source_gen=g))
                    taken += doc["n_bytes"]
                    got += 1
        report["per_generation"][g] = {"target": quota, "realized_bytes": taken, "docs": got}

    realized: dict[int, int] = {}
    for d in chosen:
        realized[d["source_gen"]] = realized.get(d["source_gen"], 0) + d["n_bytes"]
    total_realized = sum(realized.values())

    def share(dct: dict[int, int]) -> dict[int, float]:
        tot = sum(dct.values()) or 1
        return {k: v / tot for k, v in dct.items()}

    target_share = {g: weights.get(g, 0.0) / total_w for g in pools}
    realized_share = share(realized)
    keys = set(target_share) | set(realized_share)
    tvd = 0.5 * sum(abs(target_share.get(k, 0) - realized_share.get(k, 0)) for k in keys)

    report["realized_share"] = realized_share
    report["target_share"] = target_share
    report["mix_tvd"] = tvd
    report["total_bytes"] = total_realized
    return chosen, report
tests/selftest_ui.py · 2.7 KB · sha256 15fb78a4b4d0…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Uji mandiri antarmuka: memastikan metrik leksikal dihitung pada ISI, bukan markup,
dan bahwa tabel yang dikirim ke peramban memang memuat nilai yang benar.

Dijalankan `make ui-selftest` (ikut di `make check`, tidak butuh torch).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import ui_inspect as U  # noqa: E402

FAILS: list[str] = []


def check(name: str, cond: bool, detail: str = "") -> None:
    print(f"  {'OK  ' if cond else 'GAGAL'}  {name}{(' — ' + detail) if detail else ''}")
    if not cond:
        FAILS.append(name)


def main() -> int:
    print("1) markup tidak boleh menggelembungkan metrik leksikal")
    # isi = 4 token dengan 2 kata unik; markup ikut berulang di sekitarnya
    body = "kabut turun kabut turun"
    tagged = f"<D>narasi</D><P>x</P><A>{body}</A><E>"
    m = U.assess(tagged)
    check("TTR dihitung pada isi = 0,50", m["ttr"] == 0.5, f"dapat {m['ttr']}")
    check("TTR sama dengan tanpa markup", m["ttr"] == U.assess(body)["ttr"])
    # bigram isi: (kabut,turun) (turun,kabut) (kabut,turun) -> 2 unik dari 3
    check("distinct-2 isi = 0,667 (bukan efek tag)", round(m["distinct2"], 3) == 0.667, f"dapat {m['distinct2']}")
    check("distinct-2 sama dengan tanpa markup", m["distinct2"] == U.assess(body)["distinct2"])
    check("kata tidak memuat nama tag", all("<" not in str(w) for w in [k for k, _ in m["kata_terbanyak"]]),
          str(m["kata_terbanyak"]))
    check("teks berakhir <E> terdeteksi", m["berakhir_E"] is True)

    print("2) deteksi struktur rusak")
    broken = "<D>narasi</D><P>x</P><A>kabut turun"
    b = U.assess(broken)
    check("dokumen tanpa <E> -> degenerate", b["degenerate"] is True)
    check("teks pendek -> repetisi8 = n/a", b["repetisi8"] is None)

    print("3) tabel yang dikirim ke peramban memuat nilai yang benar")
    html = U.render_table({"ttr": 0.43, "repetisi8": None, "kata_terbanyak": [("kabut", 4)]}, ["arith_acc"])
    check("TTR muncul di tabel", "0.43" in html, html.count("<tr>") and "baris tabel ada")
    check("nilai kosong dirender eksplisit", "n/a" in html)
    check("daftar pasangan dirender", "kabut×4" in html)
    check("penanda lantai tidak salah tempat", html.count("di lantai") == 0)

    print("4) halaman web memuat elemen yang diperlukan")
    page = U.PAGE
    for token in ("/api/generate", "/api/assess", "table_html", "id=\"ckpt\"", "id=\"prompt\""):
        check(f"elemen {token}", token in page)

    print()
    if FAILS:
        print(f"GAGAL: {len(FAILS)} pemeriksaan -> {FAILS}")
        return 1
    print("LULUS: antarmuka dan metrik konsisten")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

tests/ui_inspect.py · 19.1 KB · sha256 75600767a139…
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Antarmuka uji manusia untuk model hasil pipeline (P0 dan seterusnya).

Tujuan: kamu bisa menilai sendiri, tanpa membaca kode dan tanpa percaya laporan,
apakah keluaran model memang memburuk antar generasi.

    python tests/ui_inspect.py            # web lokal  http://localhost:8010
    python tests/ui_inspect.py --cli      # mode terminal interaktif
    python tests/ui_inspect.py --port 9000 --bind 0.0.0.0

Fitur:
  1. Pemilihan checkpoint dari M0 baseline sampai generasi mana pun yang ada.
  2. Prompt bebas -> teks mentah yang dihasilkan model (bukan ringkasan, bukan versi bersih).
  3. Penilaian cepat: metrik yang nanti dipakai R8 (degenerate, truncated, TTR, dist-2 8-gram,
     kompresi zlib, repetisi), plus status gate checkpoint itu dari metrik mentah.

Tanpa dependensi eksternal selain torch (sudah dipakai pipeline) dan pustaka standar.
Tidak ada CDN, tidak ada aset eksternal: seluruh halaman dilayani dari proses ini.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
import re
import sys
import threading
import zlib
from collections import Counter
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

CKPT_DIRS = [ROOT / "checkpoints" / "p0", ROOT / "checkpoints" / "p1"]
METRICS_FILES = [ROOT / "runs" / "p0" / "metrics.jsonl", ROOT / "runs" / "p1" / "metrics.jsonl"]

_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_CACHE: dict[str, object] = {}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- checkpoint
def list_checkpoints() -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    meta = {}
    for mf in METRICS_FILES:
        if not mf.exists():
            continue
        for line in mf.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            row = json.loads(line)
            meta[row["model"]] = {
                "arm": row.get("arm"),
                "seed": row.get("seed"),
                "gen": row.get("gen"),
                "bpb": row.get("eval", {}).get("anchor_bpb"),
                "gate": row.get("gate", {}),
            }
    for d in CKPT_DIRS:
        if not d.exists():
            continue
        for p in sorted(d.glob("*.pt")):
            key = p.stem
            m = meta.get(key, {})
            out.append({
                "key": key,
                "size_mb": round(p.stat().st_size / 1e6, 2),
                "arm": m.get("arm", "?"),
                "seed": m.get("seed"),
                "gen": m.get("gen"),
                "bpb": m.get("bpb"),
                "gate_status": (m.get("gate") or {}).get("status"),
                "delta": (m.get("gate") or {}).get("delta_bpb"),
            })
    order = {"baseline": 0, "A": 1, "B1": 2}
    out.sort(key=lambda r: (order.get(r["arm"], 9), r.get("seed") or 0, r.get("gen") or 0))
    return out


def load_model(key: str):
    from src.model import TinyGPT
    from src.orchestrate import cfg_from

    if key in _CACHE:
        return _CACHE[key]
    path = None
    for d in CKPT_DIRS:
        cand = d / f"{key}.pt"
        if cand.exists():
            path = cand
            break
    if path is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"checkpoint {key} tidak ditemukan")
    import torch

    blob = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    model = TinyGPT(cfg_from(blob["cfg"]))
    model.load_state_dict(blob["state_dict"])
    model.eval()
    if len(_CACHE) > 3:
        _CACHE.pop(next(iter(_CACHE)))
    _CACHE[key] = model
    return model


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- metrik
def _words(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return [w.strip(".,;:!?<>/()[]{}'\"") for w in text.lower().split() if w.strip(".,;:!?<>/()[]{}'\"")]


def assess(text: str) -> dict:
    """Metrik cepat untuk teks yang baru diuji — keluarga yang sama dengan R8."""
    t = text.strip()
    # Metrik leksikal dihitung pada ISI JAWABAN saja (bagian <A>), bukan pada markup dan
    # bukan pada domain/prompt: keduanya bagian dari antarmuka, bukan keluaran model.
    # Kalau ikut dihitung, TTR/distinct-2 bisa tampak bagus hanya karena tag atau prompt berulang.
    region = t
    if "<A>" in region:
        region = region.split("<A>", 1)[1]
    if "</A>" in region:
        region = region.split("</A>", 1)[0]
    plain = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", region)
    w = _words(plain)
    grams8 = [tuple(w[i:i + 8]) for i in range(max(0, len(w) - 7))]
    bigrams = list(zip(w, w[1:]))
    raw = t.encode("utf-8")
    return {
        "byte": len(raw),
        "kata": len(w),
        "ada_A": "<A>" in t,
        "berakhir_E": t.endswith("<E>"),
        "degenerate": not ("<A>" in t and "<E>" in t),
        "ttr": round(len(set(w)) / max(1, len(w)), 3),
        "distinct2": round(len(set(bigrams)) / max(1, len(bigrams)), 3),
        "repetisi8": (round(1 - len(set(grams8)) / len(grams8), 3) if grams8 else None),
        "zlib_bpb": round(len(zlib.compress(raw, 9)) * 8 / max(1, len(raw)), 3),
        "kata_terbanyak": Counter(w).most_common(3),
        "isi_dihitung": plain.strip()[:120],
    }


def render_table(metrics: dict, floor: list[str]) -> str:
    """Tabel metrik dirender di server, supaya bisa diverifikasi tanpa menjalankan browser."""
    rows = []
    for key, val in metrics.items():
        if val is None:
            shown = "n/a (teks terlalu pendek)"
        elif isinstance(val, list):
            shown = ", ".join(f"{k}×{v}" for k, v in val) if val else "–"
        else:
            shown = str(val)
        note = '<span class="pill p-warn">di lantai</span>' if key in floor else ""
        rows.append(f"<tr><td><code>{key}</code></td><td>{shown}</td><td>{note}</td></tr>")
    return ("<table><thead><tr><th>metrik</th><th>nilai</th><th>catatan</th></tr></thead><tbody>"
            + "".join(rows) + "</tbody></table>")


def gate_summary(key: str) -> dict:
    for mf in METRICS_FILES:
        if not mf.exists():
            continue
        for line in mf.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            row = json.loads(line)
            if row["model"] == key:
                return {
                    "bpb": row["eval"]["anchor_bpb"],
                    "gate": row.get("gate", {}),
                    "eval": row.get("eval", {}),
                    "validate": row.get("validate", {}),
                }
    return {}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- inferensi
def generate(key: str, prompt: str, domain: str, temp: float, top_p: float,
             max_new: int, samples: int) -> list[str]:
    from src.generate import generate_batch

    model = load_model(key)
    prompts = [(domain, prompt)] * max(1, min(samples, 4))
    with _LOCK:
        return generate_batch(model, prompts, temp=temp, top_p=top_p, max_new=max_new)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- HTTP
PAGE = """<!doctype html>

<title>Uji model — AI Belajar dari AI</title>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
let CKPTS = [];
async function api(path, body){
  const r = await fetch(path, body ? {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body:JSON.stringify(body)} : {});
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error(await r.text());
  return await r.json();
}
function pill(s){
  const map={ok:'p-ok',watch:'p-warn',collapse:'p-bad'};
  return `<span class="pill ${map[s]||'p-mut'}">${s||'tanpa gate'}</span>`;
}
async function init(){
  CKPTS = await api('/api/checkpoints');
  const sel = $('ckpt'); sel.innerHTML='';
  if(!CKPTS.length){ sel.innerHTML='<option>tidak ada checkpoint</option>'; return; }
  for(const c of CKPTS){
    const o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=c.key;
    o.textContent = `${c.key}  ·  ${c.arm}${c.gen?(' g'+c.gen):''}  ·  bpb ${c.bpb!=null?c.bpb.toFixed(4):'?'}`;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  }
  sel.onchange = showGate; showGate();
}
function showGate(){
  const key=$('ckpt').value, c=CKPTS.find(x=>x.key===key); if(!c) return;
  $('gate').innerHTML = `<p class="muted" style="margin:10px 0 0">Gate checkpoint ini:
    ${pill(c.gate_status)} ${c.delta!=null?`Δ bpb ${c.delta>0?'+':''}${c.delta.toFixed(4)}`:''}
    · ukuran berkas ${c.size_mb} MB</p>`;
}
async function run(){
  const btn=$('go'); btn.disabled=true; $('status').textContent='menghasilkan…';
  try{
    const res = await api('/api/generate', {
      ckpt:$('ckpt').value, prompt:$('prompt').value, domain:$('domain').value,
      temp:parseFloat($('temp').value)||0.9, top_p:parseFloat($('topp').value)||0.95,
      max_new:parseInt($('maxnew').value)||120, samples:parseInt($('samples').value)||1
    });
    $('out').hidden=false;
    $('out').textContent = res.texts.map((t,i)=>`── sampel ${i+1} ──\n${t}`).join('\n\n');
    $('status').textContent = `${res.ms} ms · ${res.texts.length} sampel · ${res.n_params} parameter`;
    const m = await api('/api/assess', {ckpt:$('ckpt').value, text:res.texts[0]});
    renderMetrics(m);
  }catch(e){ $('status').textContent='gagal: '+e.message; }
  btn.disabled=false;
}
async function assessBox(){
  const t=$('out').textContent.trim();
  if(!t){ $('status').textContent='kotak hasil masih kosong'; return; }
  const m = await api('/api/assess', {ckpt:$('ckpt').value, text:t});
  renderMetrics(m);
}
function renderMetrics(m){
  $('metrics').hidden=false;
  // tabel dirender di server (render_table) sehingga tampilan tidak bergantung pada JS ini
  $('mtable').innerHTML = m.table_html || '<p class="muted">tabel tidak tersedia</p>';
  $('mnote').textContent = m.note;
}
$('go').onclick = run; $('assess').onclick = assessBox;
init().catch(e=>{ $('status').textContent='gagal memuat checkpoint: '+e.message; });
</script></body></html>
"""


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server_version = "ui_inspect/1.0"

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # ringkas, tidak spam
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype: str) -> None:
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            self._send(200, PAGE.encode(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        elif self.path == "/api/checkpoints":
            self._send(200, json.dumps(list_checkpoints()).encode(), "application/json")
        elif self.path == "/api/health":
            self._send(200, b'{"ok":true}', "application/json")
        elif self.path == "/favicon.ico":
            self._send(204, b"", "image/x-icon")
        else:
            self._send(404, b"tidak ada", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")

    def do_POST(self) -> None:
        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
        try:
            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length) or b"{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            self._send(400, b"json tidak valid", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
            return
        try:
            if self.path == "/api/generate":
                import time

                t0 = time.time()
                texts = generate(
                    payload["ckpt"], payload.get("prompt", ""), payload.get("domain", "narasi"),
                    float(payload.get("temp", 0.9)), float(payload.get("top_p", 0.95)),
                    int(payload.get("max_new", 120)), int(payload.get("samples", 1)),
                )
                model = _CACHE.get(payload["ckpt"])
                body = {
                    "texts": texts,
                    "ms": int((time.time() - t0) * 1000),
                    "n_params": getattr(model, "n_params", None),
                }
                self._send(200, json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode(), "application/json")
            elif self.path == "/api/assess":
                key = payload.get("ckpt", "")
                metrics = assess(payload.get("text", ""))
                gate = gate_summary(key)
                floor = []
                if gate.get("eval"):
                    ev = gate["eval"]
                    if ev.get("arith_n", 0) and ev.get("arith_acc", 0) <= 2 / max(1, ev["arith_n"]):
                        floor.append("arith_acc")
                note = ("Metrik 'di lantai' berarti nilai baseline-nya sudah di dasar sehingga "
                        "perubahannya tidak bisa ditafsirkan (aturan sadar-lantai R8 §6.1). "
                        "Angka di sini dihitung atas teks yang kamu uji saja, bukan atas seluruh korpus evaluasi.")
                if gate.get("validate"):
                    note += f" Reject rate korpus checkpoint ini: {gate['validate'].get('reject_rate', 0):.0%}"
                body = {"metrics": metrics, "gate": gate.get("gate", {}), "floor": floor, "note": note,
                        "table_html": render_table(metrics, floor)}
                self._send(200, json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).encode(), "application/json")
            else:
                self._send(404, b"tidak ada", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
        except Exception as exc:  # tampilkan ke pengguna, jangan telan
            self._send(500, json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode(), "application/json")


def serve(host: str, port: int) -> None:
    ck = list_checkpoints()
    print(f"checkpoint terdeteksi: {len(ck)}")
    for c in ck[:6]:
        print(f"  {c['key']:16s} arm={c['arm']:8s} gen={c['gen']} bpb={c['bpb']}")
    if len(ck) > 6:
        print(f"  … dan {len(ck) - 6} lagi")
    srv = ThreadingHTTPServer((host, port), Handler)
    print(f"\nUji model: http://localhost:{port}  (bind {host}:{port})")
    print("Tekan Ctrl+C untuk berhenti.\n")
    srv.serve_forever()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- CLI
def cli() -> None:
    ck = list_checkpoints()
    if not ck:
        print("Tidak ada checkpoint. Jalankan pipeline dulu.")
        return
    print("Checkpoint tersedia:")
    for i, c in enumerate(ck, 1):
        print(f"  [{i:2d}] {c['key']:16s} arm={c['arm']:8s} gen={c['gen']} bpb={c['bpb']}")
    idx = int(input("\nPilih nomor checkpoint: ").strip() or "1")
    key = ck[idx - 1]["key"]
    domain = input("Domain [narasi]: ").strip() or "narasi"
    while True:
        prompt = input("\nPrompt (kosong = keluar): ").strip()
        if not prompt:
            break
        temp = float(input("Suhu [0.9]: ").strip() or "0.9")
        texts = generate(key, prompt, domain, temp, 0.95, 120, 1)
        print("\n" + texts[0] + "\n")
        m = assess(texts[0])
        print("metrik:", json.dumps(m, ensure_ascii=False))
        g = gate_summary(key)
        if g:
            print(f"gate  : {g['gate'].get('status')}  Δ={g['gate'].get('delta_bpb'):+.4f}  "
                  f"ambang={g['gate'].get('threshold_bpb')}")


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Antarmuka uji manusia untuk model pipeline")
    ap.add_argument("--cli", action="store_true", help="mode terminal interaktif")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8010)
    ap.add_argument("--bind", default="0.0.0.0")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.cli:
        cli()
    else:
        serve(args.bind, args.port)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

10Hasil P0 — pilot di sandbox

Pilot P0 dijalankan pada 2 vCPU / 3 GB tanpa GPU: 1.880 dokumen T0 (199.938 byte), 4 seed baseline, 2 arm × 2 seed × 3 generasi, seluruhnya tercatat di runs/p0/metrics.jsonl. Status klaim: bukti mekanisme, bukan temuan ilmiah — 2 seed dan 3 generasi tidak punya daya statistik untuk menguji hipotesis collapse.

Laporan Pilot P0#

Status: bukti mekanisme, bukan temuan ilmiah.

P0 dijalankan di sandbox (2 vCPU, 3 GB RAM, tanpa GPU) untuk membuktikan bahwa rantai guru → T0 → M0 → generasi → validasi → training → evaluasi → gate berjalan end-to-end, dan untuk mendapatkan sigma0 pertama dari variasi antar-seed.

  • Model: XS. 481.344 parameter (byte-level. vocab 256. konteks 96)
  • Baseline M0: 4 seed -> mu0 = 0.4848 bpb, sigma0 = 0.0126 bpb
  • Chain: [('A', 13), ('A', 1337), ('B1', 13), ('B1', 1337)]
  • Langkah training per model: 600 (anggaran token identik)

20240.50080.000.9060.943

mu0 = 0.4848 bpb, sigma0 = 0.0126 bpb. Ambang R7 dihitung dari sigma0 dengan koreksi Bonferroni 180 uji dan selang prediksi (PLAN v0.3 §10.5).

Temuan P0#

1. Pipeline berjalan end-to-end, dan itu satu-satunya klaim kuat di sini#

17 node selesai tanpa intervensi manual, 16 model dilatih, 16 checkpoint tersimpan, state machine dapat di-resume (state.json + done.json), dan seluruh metrik tercatat di runs/p0/metrics.jsonl. Wall-clock 22 menit 23 detik pada 2 vCPU/3 GB tanpa GPU.

2. sigma0 pertama sudah ada — dan ambang R7 sebanding dengan efek yang kita cari#

mu0 = 0.4848 bpb, sigma0 = 0.0126 bpb (CV 2.6%), ambang R7 = 0.0488 bpb. Artinya ambang praktis 10% di atas baseline. Dengan korpus yang sangat bertemplat, baseline-nya sendiri rendah (bpb 0,48), sehingga ruang degradasi yang bisa diukur lebar.

3. Degradasi arm A jauh melampaui ambang, dan konsisten di kedua seed#

  • A generasi 1: bpb 0.9801 (delta +0.4952 = 10.2x ambang)
  • A generasi 2: bpb 1.5860 (delta +1.1012 = 22.6x ambang)
  • A generasi 3: bpb 1.9283 (delta +1.4434 = 29.6x ambang)
  • B1 generasi 1: bpb 0.7676 (delta +0.2828 = 5.8x ambang)
  • B1 generasi 3: bpb 0.9170 (delta +0.4321 = 8.9x ambang)

Semua titik sudah melewati ambang, tetapi tidak satu pun dinyatakan collapse oleh gate. Itu bukan kosmetik: ambang saja tidak cukup, dan aturan corroboration saya rusak (lihat #4).

Rasio A/B1 pada generasi 3 = 3.34x. Arah efeknya sesuai literatur (akumulasi memperlambat, bukan menghilangkan), tetapi P0 tidak punya daya untuk menguji itu: 2 seed, 3 generasi, satu keluarga korpus.

3b. Ukuran sampel yang kecil membatasi tafsir#

Evaluasi per model memakai 24 prompt generasi dan 12 item aritmetika. degenerate_rate karena itu bergerak dalam langkah 1/24 (~4,2pp) dan tidak boleh dibaca sebagai tren; arith_acc bergerak dalam langkah 1/12 (~8,3pp), sehingga aturan 'turun 10pp' hanya butuh dua item berbeda.

4. Gate saya sendiri gagal menandai collapse — ini temuan metodologis yang paling penting#

Status gate: 12 watch, 0 collapse, 0 ok dari 12 model rantai. 0 model memicu sinyal cedera kedua, padahal bpb naik sampai +1.44. Penyebabnya struktural:

  • arith_acc baseline sudah 0,00 (3 dari 4 seed) sehingga aturan 'turun >= 10pp' mustahil menyala.
  • distinct2 justru naik saat teks memburuk (0.910 -> 0.949),

karena gibberish byte-level punya lebih banyak bigram unik.

  • style_div datar (0.956 vs 0.942) — TF-IDF char-3gram tidak menangkap kerusakan sintaks.
  • repetition_rate 0,00 di semua model.

Konsekuensinya: aturan R7 + cedera-kedua under-call dalam rezim ini. Saya tidak mengubah aturan setelah melihat data (itu tepatnya yang dilarang di PLAN §10.5); sebagai gantinya P0 melahirkan kandidat R8 yang harus dipraregistrasi sebelum P1:

  1. Sinyal cedera ditambah: kenaikan degenerate_rate >= 15pp, truncated >= 20pp, dan

penurunan format_compliance (dokumen berakhir <E>) >= 20pp.

  1. Aturan sadar-lantai: bila metrik sekunder sudah di lantai (baseline 0,00) atau arahnya

tidak informatif, metrik itu dikeluarkan dari syarat corroboration, bukan dihitung sebagai 'tidak cedera'.

  1. Efek ukuran: setiap model P1 mengevaluasi minimal 40 item aritmetika agar arith_acc punya granularitas 2,5pp.

5. Model bisa menipu diri: loss turun, fidelitas jatuh#

Arm A seed 13: loss pada korpus sendiri 0.96 -> 0.76 -> 0.40, sementara anchor bpb 1.00 -> 1.73 -> 2.03. Model makin lancar meniru keluarannya sendiri dan makin jauh dari distribusi guru. Ini ilustrasi konkret mengapa evaluasi harus dari data guru yang dibekukan, bukan dari loss training.

6. Format bertahan lebih lama daripada isi#

Dokumen tanpa penutup <E>: arm A 54%/52%/48% (gen 1-3), B1 53%/24%/44%. Seluruh dokumen tetap lolos filter lenient (100% punya <D>/</P>/<A>), jadi collapse di sini bukan kegagalan format total melainkan erosi isi di dalam templat yang masih benar.

7. Yang tidak boleh diklaim dari P0#

  • Bukan bukti bahwa model 8 jt parameter akan collapse dengan laju yang sama.
  • Bukan bukti bahwa B1 aman: B1 juga sudah di atas ambang sejak generasi 1.
  • Bukan estimasi effect size: laju degradasi kemungkinan besar didorong oleh korpus templat

berukuran 200 KB dan model 0,48 jt parameter, bukan oleh sifat umum self-training.

  • Bukan uji statistik: 2 seed per arm, tanpa koreksi, tanpa blinding (D9 baru berlaku di P1).

Konsekuensi untuk P1 (belum dijalankan)#

  1. Praregistrasi R8 di PLAN sebelum P1, dengan angka di atas sebagai dasar, bukan sebagai hasil.
  1. Baseline minimal 5 seed; 3 arm (A, B1, dan kontrol tanpa self-training) x 5 seed x 6 generasi.
  1. Korpus guru non-templat minimal 2 MB, sehingga bpb baseline tidak berada di ekor rendah.
  1. Evaluasi 40+ item aritmetika dan 200+ prompt generasi per titik, agar metrik tidak di lantai.
  1. Orkestrasi dipindah ke LangGraph (saat ini state machine dengan node identik, bukan LangGraph).

Cara menjalankan ulang#

cd experiments/ai-belajar-dari-ai
python -m src.orchestrate          # idempoten: melewati node yang sudah selesai
python -m src.report_p0            # bangun ulang laporan ini + analysis/p0_metrics.csv
make check                         # lint kebijakan + build dashboard

Catatan eksekusi#

  • Semua langkah selesai: ya
  • Total model dilatih: 16
  • Total byte sintetis dihasilkan: 749.133

11Audit mandiri & protokol kegagalan

Audit ini ditulis oleh AI yang sama yang merancang sistem — jadi bobotnya lebih rendah daripada tinjauan pihak ketiga, dan itu dinyatakan di dalam dokumennya sendiri. Yang membuatnya berguna bukan retorikanya, melainkan angka yang bisa direproduksi: python3 analysis/calibration.py.
